# List 1

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abandon</b></td><td>/əˈbændən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>放纵，尽情；放弃</td><td>with reckless abandon<br>肆意放纵地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She danced with wild abandon at the wedding reception.  她在婚宴上尽情忘我地跳舞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unconstraint</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈstreɪnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无拘无束，不受约束</td><td>an air of unconstraint<br>一种无拘无束的气质</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children played with cheerful unconstraint in the empty field.  孩子们在空地上无拘无束地欢快玩耍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uninhibitedness</del></td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈhɪbɪtɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>无拘无束，不受压抑</td><td>the uninhibitedness of youth<br>青春的无拘无束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her uninhibitedness on stage charmed the entire audience.  她在台上的无拘无束让全场观众着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unrestraint</del></td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈstreɪnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>无节制，放纵</td><td>emotional unrestraint<br>情绪上的放纵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent money with an unrestraint that alarmed his family.  他花钱毫无节制，令家人担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 salvage</td><td>/ˈsælvɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>抢救，打捞</td><td>salvage the wreckage<br>打捞残骸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers tried to salvage cargo from the sunken ship.  潜水员试图从沉船中打捞货物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abase</b></td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debauch</td><td>/dɪˈbɔtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使堕落，腐化</td><td>debauch the young<br>腐化年轻人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics claimed the film would debauch impressionable young viewers.  批评者称这部电影会腐蚀易受影响的年轻观众。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 degrade</td><td>/dɪˈɡreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使降级；使退化</td><td>degrade the quality<br>降低质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such insulting jokes degrade everyone who repeats them.  这类侮辱性的笑话贬低了每个转述它们的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profane</td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitiate</td><td>/ˈvɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>削弱，损害；使无效</td><td>vitiate an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single false statement can vitiate the entire contract.  一句虚假陈述就能使整份合同失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discredit</td><td>/dɪsˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使丧失名誉；怀疑</td><td>discredit a witness<br>质疑证人可信度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer tried to discredit the witness with old records.  律师试图用旧档案质疑证人的可信度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foul</td><td>/ˈfaʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>玷污（名誉）；弄脏</td><td>foul one's good name<br>玷污自己的好名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal fouled the good name he had spent decades building.  这桩丑闻玷污了他数十年积累的好名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 smirch</del></td><td>/smɜrtʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>玷污（名誉）</td><td>smirch someone's reputation<br>玷污某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rumor smirched the reputation he had built over thirty years.  谣言玷污了他三十年建立起来的名誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 take down</td><td>/teɪk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>贬低，使丢脸；记下</td><td>take someone down a peg<br>使某人收敛傲气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reporter tried to take him down in front of the whole crowd.  记者试图在众人面前贬低他。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elevate</td><td>/ˈelɪveɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提升，提高；使兴奋</td><td>elevate the mind<br>提升心智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good literature can elevate the mind and refine one's taste.  优秀的文学能提升心智、陶冶品味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ennoble</td><td>/ɪˈnoʊbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尊贵，使高尚</td><td>ennoble the spirit<br>使精神高尚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suffering, he believed, could ennoble an ordinary life.  他相信苦难能使平凡的人生变得高贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uplift</td><td>/ʌpˈlɪft/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>振奋，鼓舞；提高</td><td>uplift the spirits<br>振奋精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cheerful letters uplifted him during the long illness.  她愉快的信在他久病期间给了他鼓舞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggrandize</td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 canonize</td><td>/ˈkænənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使神圣，封为圣徒</td><td>canonize a saint<br>封为圣徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The church voted to canonize the nun a century after her death.  教会在这位修女去世百年后决定将她封为圣徒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deify</td><td>/ˈdeɪɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>神化，奉若神明</td><td>deify a leader<br>神化领袖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The regime tried to deify its founder in every textbook.  该政权试图在每本教科书里神化其创始人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exalt</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɔːlt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞扬；提升（地位）</td><td>exalt to the skies<br>推崇到极点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics exalt her early novels as masterpieces of the era.  评论家将她早期的小说赞为那个时代的杰作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>abash</b></del></td><td>/əˈbæʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬，使羞愧</td><td>be abashed by praise<br>因受赞扬而羞怯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He looked abashed when everyone applauded his clumsy speech.  当大家为他笨拙的演讲鼓掌时，他显得很窘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discomfit</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkʌmfɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使尴尬；阻碍</td><td>discomfit an opponent<br>使对手窘迫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blunt question visibly discomfited the confident speaker.  这个直率的问题明显让自信的演讲者局促不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disconcert</td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈsɜːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使慌乱</td><td>disconcert the audience<br>使观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His long silences disconcerted everyone at the table.  他长时间的沉默让餐桌上所有人都不安。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discountenance</del></td><td>/dɪsˈkaʊntənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不赞成；使窘迫</td><td>discountenance such behavior<br>不赞成这种行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee discountenanced any further spending on the project.  委员会不赞成该项目再有任何支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faze</td><td>/feɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使慌乱，使窘迫</td><td>nothing seems to faze him<br>似乎没什么能扰乱他</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The huge crowd did not faze the young pianist at all.  庞大的观众丝毫没让这位年轻钢琴家慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluster</td><td>/ˈflʌstər/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使慌乱，使紧张</td><td>get flustered easily<br>容易慌乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unexpected questions always fluster him during interviews.  面试中意外的问题总让他慌乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 nonplus</del></td><td>/ˌnɑːnˈplʌs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使迷惑，使困窘</td><td>be completely nonplussed<br>完全不知所措</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her strange reply left the whole panel nonplussed.  她奇怪的回答让整个评审团都困惑不解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mortify</td><td>/ˈmɔːrtɪˌfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使羞愧，使难堪</td><td>be mortified with embarrassment<br>羞愧得无地自容</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was mortified to find her mistake printed in the report.  发现自己的错误印在报告里，她羞愧难当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embolden</td><td>/ɪmˈboʊldən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鼓励，使大胆</td><td>embolden the rebels<br>壮了叛乱者的胆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early success emboldened her to ask for a promotion.  初期的成功让她有胆量要求升职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abate</b></td><td>/əˈbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，减弱；减少</td><td>the storm gradually abated<br>风暴逐渐减弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fever finally abated after two days of medication.  服药两天后，烧终于退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recede</td><td>/rɪˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退去，消退，减弱</td><td>recede into the distance<br>渐渐远去消失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The floodwaters slowly receded from the village streets.  洪水缓缓从村庄的街道退去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subside</td><td>/səbˈsaɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>减弱，平息；下沉</td><td>the pain finally subsided<br>疼痛终于减轻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise subsided as soon as the teacher walked in.  老师一进来，喧闹声就平息了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 remit</td><td>/rɪˈmɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>减轻，免除；汇款</td><td>remit a penalty<br>免除罚款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge agreed to remit part of the heavy fine.  法官同意减免部分高额罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 die</td><td>/ˈdaɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减弱，消失；死亡</td><td>die down<br>渐渐平息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The applause slowly died away as the lights dimmed.  灯光变暗，掌声渐渐消失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 de-escalate</td><td>/diˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，逐步降低</td><td>de-escalate the conflict<br>缓和冲突</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides agreed to de-escalate the conflict before it turned violent.  双方同意在冲突转为暴力之前缓和局势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deplete</td><td>/dɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>耗尽，使衰竭</td><td>deplete natural resources<br>耗尽自然资源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long drought depleted the town's water reserves.  长期干旱耗尽了小镇的储水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downscale</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌskeɪl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缩减规模；低档的</td><td>downscale production<br>缩减生产规模</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company decided to downscale its overseas operations.  公司决定缩减海外业务规模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ratchet</td><td>/ˈrætʃət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>使逐步变化；棘轮</td><td>ratchet down tensions<br>逐步缓和紧张局势</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government hopes to ratchet down tensions with its neighbor.  政府希望逐步缓和与邻国的紧张关系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 augment</td><td>/ɔɡˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增加，增强</td><td>augment one's income<br>增加收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She augments her salary by teaching evening classes.  她通过教夜校课程增加收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 promote</td><td>/prəˈmoʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>促进，助长；晋升</td><td>promote economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The campaign aims to promote healthy eating among children.  该活动旨在促进儿童健康饮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abbreviate</b></td><td>/əˈbriːvieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩写，缩短</td><td>abbreviate a long title<br>缩写长标题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You may abbreviate 'Doctor' to 'Dr.' on the envelope.  信封上可以把 Doctor 缩写为 Dr.。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abridge</td><td>/əˈbrɪdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩短，删节</td><td>an abridged edition<br>删节版</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The publisher abridged the long novel for younger readers.  出版商为年轻读者删节了这部长篇小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curtail</td><td>/kɜːrˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缩短，削减</td><td>curtail public spending<br>削减公共开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bad weather curtailed our trip by three days.  坏天气使我们的旅程缩短了三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut back</td><td>/kʌt ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>削减，缩减</td><td>cut back on sugar<br>减少吃糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had to cut back on spending after the pay cut.  减薪后我们不得不削减开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 syncopate</del></td><td>/ˈsɪŋkəˌpeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>省略词中音节；使切分节奏</td><td>a syncopated rhythm<br>切分节奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Jazz players often syncopate the beat to create tension.  爵士乐手常把节拍切分以制造张力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truncate</td><td>/ˈtrʌŋkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>截短，缩短</td><td>truncate a long sentence<br>截短长句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor truncated the article to fit the page.  编辑把文章截短以适应版面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extend</td><td>/ɪkˈstend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，扩展</td><td>extend a deadline<br>延长截止期限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They agreed to extend the deadline by two weeks.  他们同意把截止日期延长两周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 protract</del></td><td>/prəˈtrækt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖长</td><td>protract the negotiations<br>拖长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless objections protracted the meeting into the evening.  无休止的反对把会议拖到了晚上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elongate</td><td>/ɪˈlɔːŋɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>拉长，延长</td><td>elongate the shape<br>拉长形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tall mirrors elongate the room and make it look larger.  高镜子拉长了房间，使它显得更大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lengthen</td><td>/ˈleŋθən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>变长，加长</td><td>lengthen a skirt<br>把裙子放长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The days lengthen noticeably after the spring equinox.  春分之后白天明显变长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 prolong</td><td>/prəˈlɔːŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖延</td><td>prolong someone's life<br>延长某人寿命</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New treatments can prolong life by several years.  新疗法能把生命延长数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abdicate</b></td><td>/ˈæbdɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>正式放弃（权力、责任）</td><td>abdicate the throne<br>退位，逊位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king chose to abdicate in favor of his younger brother.  国王选择退位让给他的弟弟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cede</td><td>/siːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃，割让</td><td>cede territory<br>割让领土</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The defeated nation was forced to cede two provinces.  战败国被迫割让两个省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relinquish</td><td>/rɪˈlɪŋkwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃（权力、职位）</td><td>relinquish control<br>放弃控制权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She refused to relinquish control of the family business.  她拒绝放弃对家族企业的控制权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resign</td><td>/rɪˈzaɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辞职，放弃（职位）</td><td>resign from office<br>辞去公职</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister resigned from office after the scandal broke.  丑闻爆发后，那位部长辞职了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 step down</td><td>/step ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辞职，下台</td><td>step down as chairman<br>辞去主席职务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He will step down as chairman at the end of the year.  他将于年底辞去董事长职务。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constitute</td><td>/ˈkɑːnstətuːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指派，设立；构成</td><td>constitute a committee<br>设立委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board was properly constituted under the new rules.  依据新规则，董事会得以正式组成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assume</td><td>/əˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>就任，承担（职权）；假定</td><td>assume office<br>就职上任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She will assume office as director next Monday.  她将于下周一就任主任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 usurp</td><td>/juːˈzɜːrp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡夺，篡位</td><td>usurp the throne<br>篡夺王位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general usurped power after the president fled.  总统逃走后，将军篡夺了权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aberrant</b></td><td>/æˈberənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，非常规的</td><td>aberrant behavior<br>异常行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors were puzzled by the patient's aberrant test results.  医生对病人异常的化验结果感到困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abnormal</td><td>/æbˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，不正常的</td><td>abnormal test results<br>异常的检测结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan showed nothing abnormal in her lungs.  扫描显示她的肺部没有异常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anomalous</td><td>/əˈnɑːmələs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不平常的，异常的</td><td>anomalous readings<br>异常的读数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team discarded a few anomalous readings from the data.  团队剔除了数据中几个异常读数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peculiar</td><td>/pəˈkjuːliər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；特有的</td><td>a peculiar smell<br>一股怪味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a peculiar smell coming from the basement.  地下室传来一股奇怪的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 singular</td><td>/ˈsɪŋɡjələr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>罕见的，非凡的；单数的</td><td>a singular achievement<br>非凡的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He showed singular courage during the long rescue.  他在漫长的营救中表现出非凡的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unwonted</del></td><td>/ʌnˈwoʊntɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不寻常的，不习惯的</td><td>unwonted silence<br>异乎寻常的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An unwonted silence fell over the noisy classroom.  嘈杂的教室里出现了不寻常的安静。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 uncustomary</del></td><td>/ʌnˈkʌstəmeri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合惯例的，罕有的</td><td>uncustomary frankness<br>少见的坦率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spoke with uncustomary frankness about his failures.  他一反常态地坦率谈起自己的失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 normal</td><td>/ˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>正常的；常态</td><td>back to normal<br>恢复正常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everything returned to normal after the power was restored.  供电恢复后一切都回归正常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 natural</td><td>/ˈnætʃərəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自然的，正常的</td><td>a natural reaction<br>自然的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is natural to feel nervous before a big exam.  大考前紧张是很自然的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 regular</td><td>/ˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>规律的，通常的</td><td>on a regular basis<br>定期地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He goes swimming on a regular basis every week.  他每周都定期去游泳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 standard</td><td>/ˈstændərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>标准的；标准，规范</td><td>standard procedure<br>标准流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Checking IDs is standard procedure at the entrance.  在入口查验证件是标准流程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 typical</td><td>/ˈtɪpəkəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>典型的，一贯的</td><td>a typical example<br>典型的例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This building is a typical example of the local style.  这栋建筑是当地风格的典型代表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abet</b></td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aid</td><td>/ˈeɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>帮助，援助</td><td>come to someone's aid<br>前来帮助某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors came to her aid within minutes of the fire.  火灾发生几分钟内邻居们就来帮忙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assist</td><td>/əˈsɪst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，协助</td><td>assist with the work<br>协助工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two nurses assisted the surgeon during the operation.  两名护士在手术中协助医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 support</td><td>/səˈpɔːrt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，支撑</td><td>support a proposal<br>支持一项提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most members voted to support the new proposal.  大多数成员投票支持这项新提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prop</td><td>/ˈprɑːp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支撑，支持；支柱</td><td>prop up a weak economy<br>扶助疲弱的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Foreign loans propped up the failing economy for years.  外国贷款支撑了衰败的经济多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stymie</td><td>/ˈstaɪmi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>stymie an investigation<br>阻碍调查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missing records stymied the investigation for months.  档案缺失使调查停滞了数月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frustrate</td><td>/ˈfrʌstreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，使受挫；使沮丧</td><td>frustrate one's plans<br>使计划受挫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow frustrated our plans to drive home.  大雪打乱了我们开车回家的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thwart</td><td>/θwɔːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻挠，挫败</td><td>thwart an attempt<br>挫败一次尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alert guards thwarted the escape attempt.  警觉的守卫挫败了这次越狱企图。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impede</td><td>/ɪmˈpiːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻碍</td><td>impede progress<br>阻碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick fog impeded the rescue team's progress.  浓雾阻碍了救援队的前进。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 forestall</td><td>/fɔːrˈstɔːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先阻止，抢先防止</td><td>forestall criticism<br>预先化解批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered early to forestall further criticism.  她提前作答以预先阻止更多批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abeyance</b></td><td>/əˈbeɪəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>中止，搁置</td><td>hold a plan in abeyance<br>暂时搁置计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The project was held in abeyance until funding arrived.  项目被搁置，直到资金到位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doldrums</td><td>/ˈdoʊldrəmz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>低迷，停滞</td><td>in the doldrums<br>处于低迷状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The housing market has been in the doldrums all year.  房地产市场整年都处于低迷状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quiescence</td><td>/kwiˈesəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>静止，休止状态</td><td>a period of quiescence<br>一段静止期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The volcano entered a long period of quiescence.  这座火山进入了长期的静止期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 moratorium</td><td>/ˌmɔːrəˈtɔːriəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>暂停，延期执行</td><td>a moratorium on hunting<br>暂停狩猎的禁令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council imposed a moratorium on new building permits.  议会暂停发放新的建筑许可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 latency</td><td>/ˈleɪtənsi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>潜伏，潜在状态；延迟</td><td>a period of latency<br>一段潜伏期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus can remain in latency for many years.  这种病毒可以潜伏多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dormancy</td><td>/ˈdɔːrmənsi/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>休眠，静止状态</td><td>break dormancy<br>打破休眠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Seeds emerge from dormancy when the soil warms.  土壤变暖时种子便结束休眠。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspension</td><td>/səˈspenʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>暂停，中止</td><td>a temporary suspension<br>暂时中止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strike led to a suspension of all train services.  罢工导致所有列车服务暂停。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cold storage</td><td>/koʊld ˈstɔrɪdʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>搁置状态；冷藏</td><td>in cold storage<br>被搁置起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal was put in cold storage after the election.  选举后该提案被搁置一旁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deep freeze</td><td>/diːp ˈfriːz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>冻结、搁置状态</td><td>in a deep freeze<br>处于冻结状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Talks between the two sides remain in a deep freeze.  双方的谈判仍处于冻结状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 continuance</td><td>/kənˈtɪnjuəns/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>继续，持续</td><td>the continuance of peace<br>和平的延续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone hoped for the continuance of the fragile ceasefire.  所有人都希望脆弱的停火能够持续。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fulfillment</td><td>/fʊlˈfɪlmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>实现，履行；满足感</td><td>the fulfillment of a promise<br>承诺的履行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty marked the fulfillment of years of patient talks.  该条约标志着多年耐心谈判的实现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abhor</b></td><td>/əbˈhɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极度厌恶，深恶痛绝</td><td>abhor violence<br>痛恨暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abhors any form of cruelty to animals.  她极度厌恶任何形式的虐待动物行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abominate</del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despise</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鄙视，极其不喜欢</td><td>despise a coward<br>鄙视懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She despised him for abandoning his closest friends.  她鄙视他抛弃至交的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detest</td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 execrate</del></td><td>/ˈeksɪkreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，咒骂</td><td>execrate a tyrant<br>痛斥暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still execrate the tyrant for his cruelty.  史学家仍因其残暴而痛斥这个暴君。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 greatly admire</td><td>/ˈɡreɪtli ədˈmaɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>非常钦佩，极为赞赏</td><td>greatly admire her courage<br>非常钦佩她的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I greatly admire the way she handled the crisis.  我非常钦佩她处理危机的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abiding</b></td><td>/əˈbaɪdɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长久的</td><td>an abiding interest<br>持久的兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has an abiding love for classical music.  她对古典音乐有着持久的热爱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ageless</td><td>/ˈeɪdʒləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永不显老的，永恒的</td><td>ageless beauty<br>不老的美貌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ageless beauty surprised everyone at the reunion.  她不显岁月的美貌让聚会上的人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enduring</td><td>/ɪnˈdʊrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>持久的，长存的</td><td>an enduring friendship<br>长久的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their enduring friendship survived decades of separation.  他们持久的友谊经历了数十年的分离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 eternal</td><td>/ɪˈtɜːrnl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>eternal life<br>永生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poem promises the beloved eternal youth.  这首诗许诺爱人永恒的青春。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 everlasting</td><td>/ˌevərˈlæstɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，持久的</td><td>everlasting peace<br>永久的和平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They vowed everlasting loyalty to each other.  他们发誓永远彼此忠诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perennial</td><td>/pəˈreniəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>长期的，反复出现的</td><td>a perennial problem<br>长期存在的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic is a perennial problem in this old city.  交通是这座老城长期存在的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetual</td><td>/pərˈpetʃuəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不断的</td><td>perpetual motion<br>永恒运动，永动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He lives in perpetual fear of losing his job.  他一直生活在失业的恐惧中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 timeless</td><td>/ˈtaɪmləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的，不受时间影响的</td><td>timeless design<br>经典不过时的设计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dress has a timeless elegance that never dates.  这条裙子有一种永不过时的优雅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 evanescent</td><td>/ˌevəˈnesnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，逐渐消失的</td><td>evanescent beauty<br>转瞬即逝的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The evanescent glow of fireflies lit the summer path.  萤火虫短暂的微光照亮了夏夜小径。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ephemeral</td><td>/ɪˈfemərəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>短暂的，转瞬即逝的</td><td>ephemeral fame<br>短暂的名声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Internet fame is often ephemeral and quickly forgotten.  网络名声往往短暂，很快被遗忘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abject</b></td><td>/ˈæbdʒekt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>悲惨的，凄凉的；卑微的</td><td>abject poverty<br>极度贫困</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Millions of people still live in abject poverty.  数百万人仍生活在极度贫困中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 base</td><td>/ˈbeɪs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑劣的，低下的</td><td>base motives<br>卑劣的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He acted from base motives of greed and envy.  他出于贪婪和嫉妒的卑劣动机行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 humble</td><td>/ˈhʌmbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑微的，地位低下的；谦逊的</td><td>humble origins<br>卑微的出身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rose from humble origins to lead the company.  他出身卑微，最终领导了这家公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 menial</td><td>/ˈmiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑贱的，低下的</td><td>menial tasks<br>低贱的杂活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took menial jobs to pay for night school.  她做低微的工作来支付夜校学费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 servile</td><td>/ˈsɜːrvl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卑屈的，奴性的</td><td>servile flattery<br>奴性的谄媚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His servile manner toward the boss annoyed everyone.  他对老板卑躬屈膝的态度让大家反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 slavish</td><td>/ˈsleɪvɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>盲从的，奴性的</td><td>slavish imitation<br>盲目的模仿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The design is a slavish imitation of an older model.  这个设计是对旧款的盲目模仿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abjure</b></td><td>/əbˈdʒʊr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃；避免</td><td>abjure violence<br>发誓弃绝暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The group publicly abjured violence as a political tool.  该团体公开宣布放弃以暴力作为政治手段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recant</td><td>/rɪˈkænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤回，公开放弃（说法）</td><td>recant a confession<br>撤回供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure he agreed to recant his earlier statement.  在压力下他同意撤回先前的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renege</td><td>/rɪˈniːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>背弃（承诺），反悔</td><td>renege on a promise<br>背弃承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reneged on its promise to raise wages.  公司背弃了加薪的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renounce</td><td>/rɪˈnaʊns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式放弃，声明弃绝</td><td>renounce one's claim<br>声明放弃权利主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He renounced his claim to the inheritance in writing.  他以书面形式放弃了继承权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 abnegate</del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forswear</td><td>/fɔrˈswer/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃，弃绝</td><td>forswear alcohol<br>发誓戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forswore gambling after losing all his savings.  输掉全部积蓄后他发誓戒赌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keep</td><td>/ˈkiːp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>遵守，坚持；保留</td><td>keep a promise<br>守约，履行承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He always keeps his promises, however inconvenient they are.  无论多不方便，他总是守约。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 affirm</td><td>/əˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>肯定，断言</td><td>affirm one's belief<br>坚定表明信念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness affirmed that she had seen the blue car.  证人确认她看见了那辆蓝色汽车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 espouse</td><td>/ɪˈspaʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，拥护</td><td>espouse a cause<br>拥护一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She openly espoused the cause of prison reform.  她公开拥护监狱改革事业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embrace</td><td>/ɪmˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>乐于接受；拥抱</td><td>embrace change<br>乐于接受变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers quickly embraced the new technology.  年轻员工迅速接受了这项新技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bow to</td><td>/ˈbaʊ tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈从于，顺从</td><td>bow to public pressure<br>屈从于公众压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government finally bowed to pressure and cancelled the new tax.  政府最终屈从于压力，取消了新税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 give in to</td><td>/ˈɡɪv ɪn tu/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于，向…让步</td><td>give in to temptation<br>屈服于诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't give in to temptation and buy another pair of shoes.  别屈服于诱惑再买一双鞋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submit to</td><td>/səbˈmɪt tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，顺从于</td><td>submit to authority<br>服从权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to submit to the will of the majority.  他拒绝服从多数人的意愿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succumb to</td><td>/səˈkʌm tu/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于；死于</td><td>succumb to pressure<br>屈服于压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks of resistance she succumbed to their demands.  抵抗数周后，她屈服于他们的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender to</td><td>/səˈrendər tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>向…投降，屈服于</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rebels finally surrendered to government forces.  叛军最终向政府军投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yield to</td><td>/ˈjiːld tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于，让位于</td><td>yield to temptation<br>经不住诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She would not yield to threats of any kind.  她不会屈服于任何形式的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>abnegate</b></del></td><td>/ˈæbnɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>放弃，否认</td><td>abnegate one's rights<br>放弃自己的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monk chose to abnegate all personal comforts.  这位僧人选择放弃一切个人享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recant</td><td>/rɪˈkænt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤回，公开放弃（说法）</td><td>recant a confession<br>撤回供词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under pressure he agreed to recant his earlier statement.  在压力下他同意撤回先前的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 renege</td><td>/rɪˈniːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>背弃（承诺），反悔</td><td>renege on a promise<br>背弃承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company reneged on its promise to raise wages.  公司背弃了加薪的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 repudiate</td><td>/rɪˈpjuːdieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>否认，拒绝接受</td><td>repudiate a claim<br>否认一项主张</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author repudiated the views quoted in the article.  作者否认了文章中引用的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsay</td><td>/ʌnˈseɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>收回（说过的话）</td><td>cannot unsay a word<br>说出的话收不回</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Once the insult is spoken, you cannot unsay it.  侮辱一旦说出，就无法收回。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forswear</td><td>/fɔrˈswer/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>发誓放弃，弃绝</td><td>forswear alcohol<br>发誓戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He forswore gambling after losing all his savings.  输掉全部积蓄后他发誓戒赌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 take back</td><td>/teɪk ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>收回（言论）；退还</td><td>take back what you said<br>收回你说的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I take back everything I said about your cooking.  我收回我说过的关于你厨艺的所有话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reaffirm</td><td>/ˌriːəˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>重申，再次确认</td><td>reaffirm one's commitment<br>重申自己的承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The president reaffirmed his commitment to the peace process.  总统重申了他对和平进程的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>abominate</b></del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despise</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鄙视，极其不喜欢</td><td>despise a coward<br>鄙视懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She despised him for abandoning his closest friends.  她鄙视他抛弃至交的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detest</td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 execrate</del></td><td>/ˈeksɪkreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，咒骂</td><td>execrate a tyrant<br>痛斥暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still execrate the tyrant for his cruelty.  史学家仍因其残暴而痛斥这个暴君。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 esteem</td><td>/ɪˈstiːm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>尊重，敬重</td><td>hold in high esteem<br>十分敬重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her colleagues esteem her for her honesty and courage.  同事们因她的诚实与勇气而敬重她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 love</td><td>/ˈlʌv/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>爱，热爱；喜爱</td><td>fall in love with<br>爱上，与…相爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I love walking along the river in the early morning.  我喜欢清晨沿着河边散步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adore</td><td>/əˈdɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热爱，极喜爱；崇拜</td><td>absolutely adore someone<br>非常喜爱某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children adore their new puppy.  孩子们非常喜爱他们的新小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aboveboard</b></td><td>/əˈbʌvbɔːrd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无欺诈的，光明正大的</td><td>open and aboveboard<br>公开而光明正大</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole deal was entirely open and aboveboard.  整笔交易完全是光明正大的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surreptitious</td><td>/ˌsɜːrəpˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偷偷摸摸的，保密的</td><td>a surreptitious glance<br>偷偷的一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a surreptitious glance at his watch during the meeting.  会上他偷偷看了一眼手表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 underhanded</td><td>/ˌʌndərˈhændɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狡诈的，暗中进行的</td><td>underhanded tactics<br>暗中使用的手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They won the contract through underhanded tactics.  他们靠不正当手段赢得了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abrade</b></td><td>/əˈbreɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>磨损，擦伤；使烦躁</td><td>abrade the skin<br>擦伤皮肤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rough rope abraded the sailor's palms.  粗糙的绳子磨伤了水手的手掌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chafe</td><td>/ˈtʃeɪf/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>摩擦致痛，磨破；恼怒</td><td>chafe against the collar<br>衣领磨得发痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stiff new boots chafed his heels until they bled.  硬邦邦的新靴子把他的脚跟磨出了血。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excoriate</td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rasp</td><td>/ˈræsp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>用粗锉磨；沙哑地说</td><td>rasp a rough edge<br>锉平粗糙的边缘</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rasped the wood smooth before painting it.  他把木头锉平后再上漆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 graze</td><td>/ˈɡreɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>擦伤，轻擦；放牧</td><td>graze one's knee<br>擦伤膝盖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She fell and grazed her knee on the gravel.  她摔倒了，膝盖在碎石上擦伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scuff</td><td>/skʌf/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）磨损</td><td>scuff one's shoes<br>磨损鞋子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He scuffed the toes of his new shoes on the steps.  他在台阶上把新鞋头磨花了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 augment</td><td>/ɔɡˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增加，增强</td><td>augment one's income<br>增加收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She augments her salary by teaching evening classes.  她通过教夜校课程增加收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abridge</b></td><td>/əˈbrɪdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩短，删节</td><td>an abridged edition<br>删节版</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The publisher abridged the long novel for younger readers.  出版商为年轻读者删节了这部长篇小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 curtail</td><td>/kɜːrˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缩短，削减</td><td>curtail public spending<br>削减公共开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bad weather curtailed our trip by three days.  坏天气使我们的旅程缩短了三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truncate</td><td>/ˈtrʌŋkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>截短，缩短</td><td>truncate a long sentence<br>截短长句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor truncated the article to fit the page.  编辑把文章截短以适应版面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cut back</td><td>/kʌt ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>削减，缩减</td><td>cut back on sugar<br>减少吃糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had to cut back on spending after the pay cut.  减薪后我们不得不削减开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extend in length</td><td>/ɪkˈstend ɪn ˈleŋθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，加长</td><td>extend in length<br>在长度上延长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge was extended in length to reach the far bank.  这座桥被加长以到达对岸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 protract</del></td><td>/prəˈtrækt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖长</td><td>protract the negotiations<br>拖长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless objections protracted the meeting into the evening.  无休止的反对把会议拖到了晚上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amplify</td><td>/ˈæmpləˌfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大，增强；详述</td><td>amplify a signal<br>放大信号</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you amplify your last remark a little?  你能把最后那句话再详述一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abrogate</b></td><td>/ˈæbrəɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>正式废除（法律、条约）</td><td>abrogate a treaty<br>废除条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Parliament voted to abrogate the outdated law.  议会投票废除这项过时的法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disannul</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈnʌl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>disannul a contract<br>废除合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court moved to disannul the fraudulent agreement.  法院着手废除这份欺诈协议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 invalidate</td><td>/ɪnˈvælɪdeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，使作废</td><td>invalidate a contract<br>使合同无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A missing signature will invalidate the whole form.  缺少签名会使整张表格失效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nullify</td><td>/ˈnʌləfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使无效，废除</td><td>nullify an agreement<br>使协议失效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge nullified the election result.  法官宣布选举结果无效。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 quash</td><td>/kwɔːʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>撤销，废除；镇压，阻止</td><td>quash a conviction<br>撤销有罪判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeal court quashed his conviction last week.  上诉法院上周撤销了对他的定罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rescind</td><td>/rɪˈsɪnd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>废除，取消</td><td>rescind an offer<br>撤回报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company rescinded the job offer without explanation.  公司未加解释便撤回了聘用通知。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roll back</td><td>/roʊl ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>撤销，削减</td><td>roll back regulations<br>撤销法规</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new administration promised to roll back the tax cuts.  新政府承诺撤销这些减税措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 strike down</td><td>/straɪk ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>判定（法律）无效；击倒</td><td>strike down a law<br>判定某法律无效</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court decided to strike down the law as unconstitutional.  法院裁定该法律违宪而将其废除。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 embrace</td><td>/ɪmˈbreɪs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>乐于接受；拥抱</td><td>embrace change<br>乐于接受变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Young workers quickly embraced the new technology.  年轻员工迅速接受了这项新技术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uphold</td><td>/ʌpˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，赞成；维持（原判）</td><td>uphold a decision<br>维持原判决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The appeals court chose to uphold the original ruling in full.  上诉法院决定完全维持原判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 institute</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪtuːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>创立，制定；机构，学院</td><td>institute legal proceedings<br>提起法律诉讼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school instituted a new dress code this term.  学校本学期制定了新的着装规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abscond</b></td><td>/əbˈskɑːnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>偷偷离开，潜逃</td><td>abscond with the funds<br>携款潜逃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treasurer absconded with thousands of dollars.  财务主管带着数千美元潜逃了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flee</td><td>/ˈfliː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>逃离，逃走</td><td>flee the country<br>逃离这个国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands fled the city as the fighting spread.  战火蔓延时数千人逃离该市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lam</td><td>/ˈlæm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>逃跑，潜逃</td><td>on the lam<br>在逃亡之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief has been on the lam for two months.  这名窃贼已潜逃两个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 run off</td><td>/rʌn ˈɔf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>逃跑，私奔</td><td>run off with someone<br>与某人私奔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She ran off with her childhood sweetheart just before the wedding.  她在婚礼前和儿时的恋人私奔了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 break out</td><td>/breɪk ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>越狱，逃脱；突然爆发</td><td>break out of prison<br>越狱逃跑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two prisoners managed to break out of the jail last night.  两名囚犯昨晚成功越狱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>absolute</b></td><td>/ˈæbsəluːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专制的；无限的；纯净不掺杂的；确凿的</td><td>absolute power<br>绝对的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king held absolute power over his subjects.  国王对臣民拥有绝对权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arbitrary</td><td>/ˈɑːrbətreri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专横的，独断专行的；随意的</td><td>an arbitrary decision<br>武断的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rules seemed completely arbitrary to the students.  这些规定在学生看来完全是随意的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 autocratic</td><td>/ˌɔːtəˈkrætɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>独裁的，专制的</td><td>an autocratic ruler<br>专制的统治者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His autocratic style alienated the entire staff.  他专断的作风疏远了全体员工。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 despotic</td><td>/dɪˈspɑːtɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>专制的，暴虐的</td><td>a despotic regime<br>专制的政权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The country suffered under a despotic regime for decades.  这个国家在专制政权下受苦数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dictatorial</td><td>/ˌdɪktəˈtɔːriəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>独裁的；专横的</td><td>a dictatorial manner<br>专横的作风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach's dictatorial manner made several players quit.  教练专横的态度让几名球员退出了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 monocratic</del></td><td>/ˌmɑnəˈkrætɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>独裁的，一人统治的</td><td>a monocratic state<br>一人独裁的国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constitution replaced monocratic rule with an elected assembly.  宪法用选举议会取代了独裁统治。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tyrannical</td><td>/təˈrænɪkəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>暴虐的，专制的</td><td>a tyrannical father<br>暴虐的父亲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was a tyrannical boss who trusted no one.  他是个不信任任何人的暴虐老板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complete</td><td>/kəmˈpliːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完全的，彻底的；完成</td><td>a complete failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first attempt was a complete failure.  第一次尝试彻底失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 utter</td><td>/ˈʌtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完全的，彻底的；说出</td><td>utter nonsense<br>一派胡言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he said was utter nonsense from start to finish.  他说的从头到尾都是彻底的胡话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deadly</td><td>/ˈdedli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>十足的，完全的；致命的</td><td>deadly serious<br>极其严肃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was deadly serious about quitting her job.  她对辞职的事完全是认真的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 downright</td><td>/ˈdaʊnˌraɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，彻底的</td><td>downright dangerous<br>十足危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That suggestion is downright dangerous and should be dropped.  那个建议完全是危险的，应该放弃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 out-and-out</td><td>/ˌaʊt ənd ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>十足的，彻底的</td><td>an out-and-out lie<br>彻头彻尾的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he told the committee was an out-and-out lie.  他对委员会说的完全是弥天大谎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plain</td><td>/ˈpleɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>完全的，十足的；朴素的，清楚的</td><td>plain stupid<br>简直愚蠢</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning was just plain stupid.  忽视警告纯粹是愚蠢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refined</td><td>/rəˈfaɪnd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精炼的，纯净的；有教养的</td><td>refined sugar<br>精制糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Refined sugar has been stripped of every impurity.  精制糖已去除了所有杂质。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unadulterated</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈdʌltəreɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>纯净的，未掺杂的</td><td>unadulterated joy<br>纯粹的喜悦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her face showed unadulterated delight at the news.  听到这消息她脸上是纯粹的喜悦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unalloyed</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈlɔɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>纯粹的，无掺杂的</td><td>unalloyed pleasure<br>纯粹的快乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trip was an unalloyed pleasure from start to finish.  这次旅行从头到尾都是纯粹的享受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 undiluted</td><td>/ˌʌndaɪˈluːtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未稀释的；纯粹的</td><td>undiluted juice<br>未稀释的果汁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You should never drink this cough syrup undiluted.  你绝不该喝未稀释的止咳糖浆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unmixed</td><td>/ˌʌnˈmɪkst/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未混合的，纯粹的</td><td>an unmixed blessing<br>有利无弊的好事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fame proved to be an unmixed curse for him.  名声对他来说是纯粹的祸事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear</td><td>/ˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，确凿的；清楚的</td><td>a clear majority<br>明显的多数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The party won by a clear majority of votes.  该党以明显多数票获胜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deciding</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪdɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的</td><td>the deciding factor<br>决定性的因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was the deciding factor in our choice.  成本是我们选择的决定性因素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 decisive</td><td>/dɪˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>决定性的；果断的</td><td>a decisive victory<br>决定性的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her testimony was decisive in the trial.  她的证词在审判中起了决定性作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 definitive</td><td>/dɪˈfɪnɪtɪv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>确定的，不容置疑的；权威的</td><td>a definitive answer<br>确定无疑的答案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We still have no definitive answer from the committee.  我们仍未从委员会得到确定的答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 qualified</td><td>/ˈkwɑːləˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有限制的，有保留的；合格的</td><td>qualified approval<br>有保留的赞同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan received only qualified approval from the board.  该计划仅获得董事会有保留的认可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adulterated</td><td>/əˈdʌltəreɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>掺假的，不纯的</td><td>adulterated milk<br>掺假的牛奶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors found adulterated milk on the shelves.  检查人员在货架上发现掺假牛奶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alloyed</td><td>/ˈælɔɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>掺有杂质的，合金化的</td><td>alloyed metal<br>掺有杂质的金属</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pure gold is soft, so it is usually alloyed with copper.  纯金较软，所以通常掺铜制成合金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diluted</td><td>/daɪˈluːtəd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀释的；削弱的</td><td>heavily diluted paint<br>严重稀释的油漆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paint was too heavily diluted to cover the wall.  漆稀释得太厉害，盖不住墙面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impure</td><td>/ɪmˈpjʊr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不纯的，掺杂的</td><td>impure water<br>不洁的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sample was impure and had to be discarded.  样本不纯，只能丢弃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mixed</td><td>/ˈmɪkst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>混合的，掺杂的；好坏交织的</td><td>mixed feelings<br>复杂的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I have mixed feelings about moving abroad.  对于出国我心情复杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconclusive</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈkluːsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有定论的</td><td>inconclusive evidence<br>无定论的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tests were inconclusive, so they ran them again.  检测没有定论，于是他们又做了一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indecisive</td><td>/ˌɪndɪˈsaɪsɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>犹豫不决的；非决定性的</td><td>hopelessly indecisive<br>极其优柔寡断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is too indecisive to lead the team.  他太犹豫不决，无法带领团队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unclear</td><td>/ʌnˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不清楚的，不明确的</td><td>remain unclear<br>仍不明确</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is still unclear who made the final decision.  谁做的最终决定仍不清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>absolve</b></td><td>/əbˈzɑːlv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使无罪，解除责任</td><td>absolve someone of blame<br>免除某人的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report absolved the pilot of all blame.  报告免除了飞行员的全部责任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquit</td><td>/əˈkwɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣判无罪</td><td>acquit of murder<br>宣判谋杀罪不成立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The jury acquitted him of all charges.  陪审团宣判他所有罪名不成立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exonerate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzɑːnəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>免除责备</td><td>exonerate from blame<br>免除责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence exonerated the wrongly jailed man.  新证据为这名被误判入狱者洗清了罪名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vindicate</td><td>/ˈvɪndɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>为…平反，为…辩护，使无罪；证明，证实</td><td>vindicate one's reputation<br>为自己的名誉洗冤</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The results vindicated her unusual approach.  结果证明了她那不寻常的方法是对的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blame</td><td>/ˈbleɪm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>责备，归咎于</td><td>blame someone for something<br>因某事责怪某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't blame me for the broken window.  别把窗户破了怪在我头上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 criminate</del></td><td>/ˈkrɪməneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>指控，归罪于</td><td>criminate an accomplice<br>指控一名同伙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His own letters criminated him at the trial.  他自己的信件在审判中给他定了罪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incriminate</td><td>/ɪnˈkrɪmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>归罪于</td><td>incriminate oneself<br>使自己受牵连</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to answer questions that might incriminate him.  他拒绝回答可能使自己入罪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abstain</b></td><td>/əbˈsteɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>自我克制，主动戒绝</td><td>abstain from alcohol<br>戒酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Three members abstained from voting on the motion.  三名成员对该动议投了弃权票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forgo</td><td>/fɔːrˈɡoʊ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放弃，弃绝</td><td>forgo a salary<br>放弃薪水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agreed to forgo her bonus this year.  她同意放弃今年的奖金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keep from</td><td>/ˈkiːp frəm/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>克制不做，避免；不让…知道</td><td>keep from laughing<br>忍住不笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I could hardly keep from laughing at his hat.  我几乎忍不住对他的帽子笑出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 refrain from</td><td>/rɪˈfreɪn frəm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>忍住不做，戒除</td><td>refrain from smoking<br>忍住不吸烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please refrain from using your phone during the film.  观影期间请勿使用手机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withhold</td><td>/wɪðˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制；扣压，不给予</td><td>withhold information<br>隐瞒信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of withholding key information.  他被指控隐瞒关键信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bow to</td><td>/ˈbaʊ tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈从于，顺从</td><td>bow to public pressure<br>屈从于公众压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government finally bowed to pressure and cancelled the new tax.  政府最终屈从于压力，取消了新税。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 give in to</td><td>/ˈɡɪv ɪn tu/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于，向…让步</td><td>give in to temptation<br>屈服于诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't give in to temptation and buy another pair of shoes.  别屈服于诱惑再买一双鞋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 submit to</td><td>/səbˈmɪt tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，顺从于</td><td>submit to authority<br>服从权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to submit to the will of the majority.  他拒绝服从多数人的意愿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succumb to</td><td>/səˈkʌm tu/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于；死于</td><td>succumb to pressure<br>屈服于压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After weeks of resistance she succumbed to their demands.  抵抗数周后，她屈服于他们的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 surrender to</td><td>/səˈrendər tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>向…投降，屈服于</td><td>surrender to the police<br>向警方投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rebels finally surrendered to government forces.  叛军最终向政府军投降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yield to</td><td>/ˈjiːld tu/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>屈服于，让位于</td><td>yield to temptation<br>经不住诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She would not yield to threats of any kind.  她不会屈服于任何形式的威胁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abstemious</b></td><td>/əbˈstiːmiəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（吃喝等）有节制的，节俭的</td><td>an abstemious diet<br>有节制的饮食</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He led an abstemious life, eating little and drinking less.  他生活节制，吃得少喝得更少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 continent</td><td>/ˈkɑːntənənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>自我节制的，禁欲的；大陆</td><td>remain continent<br>保持禁欲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The monks were expected to be continent in all appetites.  僧侣们应在一切欲望上自我节制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 temperate</td><td>/ˈtempərət/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言行举止）有分寸的，有节制的</td><td>a temperate response<br>有分寸的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave a temperate reply to a very rude question.  面对无礼的问题她的回答很有分寸。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 self-abnegating</del></td><td>/ˌself ˈæbnəɡeɪtɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>克己的，自我牺牲的</td><td>self-abnegating devotion<br>克己的奉献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her self-abnegating care for others left no time for herself.  她克己地照顾他人，无暇顾及自己。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-denying</td><td>/ˌself dɪˈnaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>克己的，自我节制的</td><td>a self-denying habit<br>克己的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His self-denying routine allowed no sweets at all.  他克己的作息完全不允许甜食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indulgent</td><td>/ɪnˈdʌldʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（对己）放纵的，（对他人）纵容的</td><td>an indulgent parent<br>溺爱孩子的家长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her indulgent grandparents let her eat whatever she wanted.  溺爱她的祖父母让她想吃什么就吃什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abstract</b></td><td>/æbˈstrækt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>做总结，概括；使分心</td><td>abstract a long report<br>概括一份长报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abstracted the key findings into a single page.  她把主要发现概括成一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 digest</td><td>/daɪˈdʒest/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>领会，概括；消化</td><td>digest the information<br>领会这些信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me a moment to digest what you just said.  给我点时间消化你刚说的话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recapitulate</td><td>/ˌriːkəˈpɪtʃuleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>概括，摘要</td><td>recapitulate the main points<br>概括要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let me recapitulate the main points before we vote.  投票前让我概括一下要点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 synopsize</del></td><td>/sɪˈnɑpsaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>概述，做提要</td><td>synopsize a novel<br>概述一部小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Can you synopsize the plot in two sentences?  你能用两句话概述剧情吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sum up</td><td>/sʌm ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>总结，概括</td><td>sum up the argument<br>总结论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 To sum up, the project needs more time and money.  总之，这个项目需要更多时间和资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boil down</td><td>/bɔɪl ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>归结为，浓缩概括</td><td>boil down to one issue<br>归结为一个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It all boils down to a question of trust.  这一切归结为信任问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detract</td><td>/dɪˈtrækt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低价值；使分心</td><td>detract from the value<br>降低其价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ugly frame detracts from the painting.  难看的画框降低了这幅画的价值。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 divert</td><td>/daɪˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使转向；使消遣</td><td>divert attention from<br>转移对…的注意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise diverted my attention from the lecture.  噪音把我的注意力从讲座上引开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 call off</td><td>/kɔl ˈɔf/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>取消；叫走，使离开</td><td>call off the search<br>取消搜寻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They called off the search because of the storm.  因暴风雨他们取消了搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 throw off</td><td>/θroʊ ˈɔf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使分心，扰乱；摆脱</td><td>throw someone off balance<br>使某人失去平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden question threw him off and he lost his train of thought.  这个突然的问题打乱了他，让他忘了要说什么。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 elaborate</td><td>/ɪˈlæbəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>详细阐述；精心制作的</td><td>elaborate on a point<br>详述某一点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Could you elaborate on your last point a little?  你能就最后一点再详细说明一下吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 attention</td><td>/əˈtenʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>注意力，专注</td><td>pay attention to<br>注意，留意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please pay attention to the safety instructions.  请注意安全说明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abstruse</b></td><td>/əbˈstruːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>abstruse theories<br>深奥的理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lecture was too abstruse for first-year students.  他的讲座对一年级学生来说太晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arcane</td><td>/ɑːrˈkeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的，难以理解的</td><td>arcane rules<br>晦涩难懂的规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax code is full of arcane rules nobody understands.  税法充满无人理解的晦涩规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 esoteric</td><td>/ˌesəˈterɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥难懂的</td><td>esoteric knowledge<br>深奥的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She writes about esoteric topics in medieval music.  她写中世纪音乐中深奥的题目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hermetic</td><td>/hərˈmetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深奥的；密封的</td><td>a hermetic style<br>晦涩的风格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The poet's hermetic style baffles most readers.  这位诗人晦涩的风格令多数读者困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 accessible</td><td>/əkˈsesəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可以到达的；可理解的</td><td>an accessible explanation<br>易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes physics accessible to ordinary readers.  他让普通读者也能理解物理学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shallow</td><td>/ˈʃæloʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浅薄的，肤浅的；浅的</td><td>shallow water<br>浅水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arguments are shallow and easy to refute.  他的论点浅薄，很容易反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 superficial</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>a superficial understanding<br>肤浅的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has only a superficial knowledge of the subject.  她对这门学科只有肤浅的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>absurd</b></td><td>/əbˈsɜːrd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不合理的，荒谬的</td><td>an absurd suggestion<br>荒谬的建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It's absurd to expect results in a single week.  期望一周内出结果是荒谬的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wild</td><td>/ˈwaɪld/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，毫无根据的；野生的</td><td>a wild guess<br>瞎猜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That's a wild guess with no evidence behind it.  那是毫无根据的胡乱猜测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bizarre</td><td>/bəˈzɑːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怪异的，离奇的</td><td>a bizarre coincidence<br>离奇的巧合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 By some bizarre coincidence we wore the same coat.  出于某种离奇的巧合，我们穿了同款外套。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ludicrous</td><td>/ˈluːdɪkrəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，可笑的</td><td>a ludicrous idea<br>可笑的想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The price they asked was simply ludicrous.  他们的要价简直可笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insane</td><td>/ɪnˈseɪn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒唐的，疯狂的</td><td>an insane plan<br>疯狂的计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Driving in this thick fog would be insane.  在这种大雾里开车太疯狂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 nonsensical</td><td>/nɑːnˈsensɪkəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒谬的，无意义的</td><td>a nonsensical argument<br>荒谬的论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were nonsensical and helped nobody.  说明荒谬无理，对谁都没帮助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preposterous</td><td>/prɪˈpɑːstərəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>荒谬的，极其反常的</td><td>a preposterous claim<br>荒唐的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The idea that he acted alone is preposterous.  说他独自行动的说法荒谬至极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 half-baked</td><td>/ˌhæfˈbeɪkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不成熟的，欠考虑的</td><td>a half-baked scheme<br>不成熟的方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed ahead with a half-baked plan.  他们仓促推行了一个不成熟的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rational</td><td>/ˈræʃnəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>理性的，合理的</td><td>a rational decision<br>理性的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Try to make a rational decision instead of an emotional one.  试着做理性而非情绪化的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 realistic</td><td>/ˌriːəˈlɪstɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>现实的，切合实际的</td><td>a realistic goal<br>切合实际的目标</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let's set a realistic goal for this month.  我们为本月定一个切合实际的目标吧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reasonable</td><td>/ˈriːzənəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>合理的，通情达理的</td><td>a reasonable request<br>合理的请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That seems like a perfectly reasonable request.  那似乎是完全合理的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abundant</b></td><td>/əˈbʌndənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，丰富的</td><td>abundant evidence<br>大量的证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water was abundant after the spring rains.  春雨过后水源充足。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ample</td><td>/ˈæmpəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充足的，富裕的</td><td>ample opportunity<br>充足的机会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is ample room for everyone in the van.  车里有足够的空间容纳所有人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cornucopian</del></td><td>/ˌkɔrnəˈkoʊpiən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>丰富的，充裕的</td><td>cornucopian abundance<br>极为丰饶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered a cornucopian display of fruit.  市场上摆着丰富多样的水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 teeming</td><td>/ˈtiːmɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>大量的，充满的</td><td>teeming with life<br>充满生机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pond was teeming with tadpoles in May.  五月池塘里满是蝌蚪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 replete</td><td>/rɪˈpliːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满…的，富于…的</td><td>replete with errors<br>错误连篇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His essay was replete with spelling errors.  他的文章满是拼写错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abounding</td><td>/əˈbaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满的，大量的</td><td>abounding in fish<br>盛产鱼类</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They found a river abounding in trout.  他们发现一条鳟鱼很多的河。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infrequent</td><td>/ˌɪnˈfriːkwənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀少的，不常发生的</td><td>infrequent visits<br>难得的探访</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His visits home became increasingly infrequent.  他回家的次数越来越少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rare</td><td>/ˈrer/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀少的，罕见的</td><td>a rare bird<br>罕见的鸟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is rare to see snow here in April.  四月这里下雪很罕见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uncommon</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不常见的，罕有的</td><td>not uncommon<br>并不罕见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such mistakes are not uncommon among beginners.  这类错误在初学者中并不罕见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inadequate</td><td>/ɪnˈædəkwət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不充足的，不胜任的</td><td>inadequate funding<br>资金不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lighting in the room is completely inadequate.  房间的照明完全不够。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scanty</td><td>/ˈskænti/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏的，稀少的</td><td>scanty evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Evidence for the theory remains scanty.  支持该理论的证据仍然很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scarce</td><td>/ˈskers/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>稀缺的，不足的</td><td>in scarce supply<br>供应短缺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fresh water became scarce during the drought.  旱灾期间淡水变得稀缺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abuse</b></td><td>/əˈbjuːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>辱骂，抨击；不正当或不合理使用</td><td>abuse of power<br>滥用权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of abusing his position for profit.  他被指控滥用职位谋利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assail</td><td>/əˈseɪl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>assail a policy<br>抨击某项政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Opponents assailed the plan as reckless and unfair.  反对者抨击该计划轻率且不公。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bash</td><td>/bæʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，严厉批评</td><td>bash the media<br>抨击媒体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians love to bash the press at rallies.  政客们喜欢在集会上抨击新闻界。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excoriate</td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lambaste</td><td>/læmˈbeɪst/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉斥责，痛骂</td><td>lambaste the government<br>痛斥政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics lambasted the film for its shallow, sentimental ending.  批评家严厉斥责这部电影结局浅薄而煽情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 misemploy</del></td><td>/ˌmɪsɪmˈplɔɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>误用，滥用</td><td>misemploy public funds<br>滥用公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials were accused of misemploying money meant for schools.  官员们被指控滥用本应用于学校的资金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 misuse</td><td>/ˌmɪsˈjuːs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>滥用，误用</td><td>the misuse of power<br>滥用权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The misuse of antibiotics has created dangerous resistant bacteria.  滥用抗生素造成了危险的耐药细菌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acquainted</b></td><td>/əˈkweɪntɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，认识的</td><td>be acquainted with<br>熟悉；与……相识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I am not well acquainted with the rules of this game.  我对这个游戏的规则不太熟悉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 familiar</td><td>/fəˈmɪljər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>familiar with the subject<br>熟悉这门学科</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice sounded strangely familiar over the phone.  她的声音在电话里听起来奇怪地熟悉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conversant</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrsnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟悉的，通晓的</td><td>conversant with the law<br>通晓法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is fully conversant with the new safety regulations.  她非常熟悉新的安全条例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knowledgeable</td><td>/ˈnɑːlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>知识丰富的，了解很多的</td><td>knowledgeable about wine<br>懂葡萄酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our guide was remarkably knowledgeable about local birds.  我们的导游对当地鸟类了解得非常多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 informed</td><td>/ɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见识广的，掌握信息的</td><td>make an informed decision<br>做出明智的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Voters need accurate data to make an informed choice.  选民需要准确的数据才能做出明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versed</td><td>/vɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟知的；精通的</td><td>well versed in history<br>精通历史</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is well versed in both classical and modern poetry.  他对古典诗歌和现代诗歌都很精通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-informed</td><td>/ˌwel ɪnˈfɔrmd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>见闻广博的，消息灵通的</td><td>a well-informed source<br>消息可靠的来源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A well-informed reader spotted the error in the report.  一位见闻广博的读者发现了报告中的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ignorant</td><td>/ˈɪɡnərənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知的，不知道的</td><td>ignorant of the facts<br>不了解事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They were completely ignorant of the danger ahead.  他们对前方的危险毫不知情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unacquainted</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈkweɪntɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟悉的，不了解的</td><td>unacquainted with the area<br>不熟悉这个地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Being unacquainted with the city, he got lost very quickly.  由于不熟悉这座城市，他很快就迷路了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfamiliar</td><td>/ˌʌnfəˈmɪljər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟悉的，陌生的</td><td>an unfamiliar face<br>一张陌生的脸</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The street names here were all unfamiliar to me.  这里的街名对我来说都很陌生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 uninformed</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈfɔːrmd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不了解情况的，无知的</td><td>an uninformed opinion<br>不明情况的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Uninformed criticism rarely helps anyone improve their work.  不了解情况的批评很少能帮助别人改进工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
