<tr><td><del>↳反 unknowledgeable</del></td><td>/ˌʌnˈnɑlɪdʒəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏知识的，不懂行的</td><td>unknowledgeable about finance<br>不懂金融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt unknowledgeable about finance and hired an adviser.  他觉得自己不懂金融，于是聘请了一位顾问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>abysmal</b></td><td>/əˈbɪzməl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>糟透的，极差的；深不可测的</td><td>abysmal ignorance<br>极度无知</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team's performance this season has been simply abysmal.  这个赛季球队的表现简直糟糕透顶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profound</td><td>/proʊˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深远的</td><td>a profound influence<br>深远的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her death had a profound effect on the whole family.  她的死对整个家庭产生了深远的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shallow</td><td>/ˈʃæloʊ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浅薄的，肤浅的；浅的</td><td>shallow water<br>浅水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His arguments are shallow and easy to refute.  他的论点浅薄，很容易反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shoal</td><td>/ʃoʊl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浅的</td><td>shoal water ahead<br>前方水浅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The captain warned us about the shoal water near the reef.  船长提醒我们注意礁石附近的浅水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 skin-deep</td><td>/ˌskɪn ˈdiːp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>beauty is only skin-deep<br>美貌只是表面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His interest in the subject turned out to be skin-deep.  他对这个题目的兴趣原来只是浮于表面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 superficial</td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈfɪʃl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表面的，肤浅的</td><td>a superficial understanding<br>肤浅的理解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has only a superficial knowledge of the subject.  她对这门学科只有肤浅的了解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accede</b></td><td>/əkˈsiːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>同意，答应；就任</td><td>accede to a request<br>答应请求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government finally acceded to the union's demands.  政府最终同意了工会的要求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acquiesce</td><td>/ˌækwiˈes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>勉强同意；默许</td><td>acquiesce in the decision<br>默许该决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She acquiesced in their plan rather than start an argument.  她默许了他们的计划，以免引起争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consent</td><td>/kənˈsent/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>give one's consent<br>表示同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children cannot travel without their parents' consent.  没有父母的同意，孩子们不能出行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subscribe</td><td>/səbˈskraɪb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>赞同（观点）；订阅</td><td>subscribe to a view<br>赞同某观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not subscribe to the view that money buys happiness.  我不赞同金钱能买到幸福的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 come round</td><td>/kʌm ˈraʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>改变主意而同意；苏醒</td><td>come round to an idea<br>转而接受某想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took weeks, but she finally came round to our plan.  过了几周，她终于转变态度，同意了我们的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 specific</td><td>/spəˈsɪfɪk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，特定的；专门的</td><td>a specific example<br>一个具体的例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Can you give me a specific example of the problem?  你能给我一个这个问题的具体例子吗？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 specified</td><td>/ˈspesəˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>规定的，指定的</td><td>at the specified time<br>在规定的时间</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please return the completed form by the specified deadline.  请在规定的截止日期前交回填好的表格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demur</td><td>/dɪˈmɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>表示异议，反对</td><td>demur at the suggestion<br>对建议表示异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She demurred at the suggestion that she should resign.  对于让她辞职的建议，她表示了异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissent</td><td>/dɪˈsent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>持异议，不同意；反对正统</td><td>voice one's dissent<br>表达异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two judges dissented from the majority opinion of the court.  两位法官对法院的多数意见持异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ad hoc</td><td>/ˌæd ˈhɑk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>临时的，专门的</td><td>an ad hoc committee<br>临时委员会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They set up an ad hoc committee to handle the crisis.  他们成立了一个临时委员会来应对这场危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accessible</b></td><td>/əkˈsesəbl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可以到达的；可理解的</td><td>an accessible explanation<br>易懂的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He makes physics accessible to ordinary readers.  他让普通读者也能理解物理学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 handy</td><td>/ˈhændi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>方便的；就在手边的</td><td>come in handy<br>派上用场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep a flashlight handy in case the power fails tonight.  把手电筒放在手边，以防今晚停电。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reachable</td><td>/ˈriːtʃəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可到达的；可联系上的</td><td>reachable by phone<br>可电话联系到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village is reachable only by a narrow mountain road.  这个村子只能通过一条狭窄的山路到达。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 apprehensible</del></td><td>/ˌæprɪˈhensəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的</td><td>readily apprehensible ideas<br>易于理解的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument is barely apprehensible to readers without training.  他的论证对没有受过训练的读者几乎难以理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fathomable</del></td><td>/ˈfæðəməbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可理解的，可探明的</td><td>hardly fathomable motives<br>难以捉摸的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her reasons for leaving were not easily fathomable to us.  她离开的原因我们不容易理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 legible</td><td>/ˈledʒəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>字迹清楚的，易辨认的</td><td>barely legible handwriting<br>几乎难以辨认的字迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His handwriting is so small that it is hardly legible.  他的字太小，几乎难以辨认。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scrutable</del></td><td>/ˈskruːtəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可以理解的</td><td>scrutable motives<br>可探明的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Few of his decisions were scrutable to the junior staff.  他的决定很少能被初级员工理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconvenient</td><td>/ˌɪnkənˈviːniənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不方便的，造成麻烦的</td><td>at an inconvenient time<br>在不方便的时候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She called at an inconvenient time, just before dinner.  她在不方便的时候打来电话，正好在晚饭前。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unhandy</del></td><td>/ʌnˈhændi/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不便使用的；笨手笨脚的</td><td>an unhandy tool<br>不好用的工具</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This unhandy old ladder is difficult to carry upstairs.  这把不好用的旧梯子很难搬上楼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unreachable</td><td>/ˌʌnˈriːtʃəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法到达的；联系不上的</td><td>unreachable by phone<br>电话联系不上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was unreachable all weekend because of the poor signal.  由于信号不好，他整个周末都联系不上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 untouchable</td><td>/ˌʌnˈtʌtʃəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可接触的；不可动摇的</td><td>considered untouchable<br>被视为不可动摇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Senior managers at the firm seemed completely untouchable.  这家公司的高层管理者似乎完全碰不得。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abstruse</td><td>/əbˈstruːs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>abstruse theories<br>深奥的理论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lecture was too abstruse for first-year students.  他的讲座对一年级学生来说太晦涩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incoherent</td><td>/ˌɪnkoʊˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>语无伦次的，不连贯的</td><td>an incoherent argument<br>前后不连贯的论证</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was so upset that his explanation became incoherent.  他太难受了，解释变得语无伦次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incomprehensible</td><td>/ˌɪnkɑːmprɪˈhensəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法理解的，难懂的</td><td>utterly incomprehensible instructions<br>完全无法理解的说明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The instructions were incomprehensible to a first-time user.  这些说明对第一次使用的人来说无法理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inscrutable</td><td>/ɪnˈskruːtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>高深莫测的，难以理解的</td><td>an inscrutable expression<br>高深莫测的表情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inscrutable smile gave nothing away about his plans.  他那高深莫测的笑容丝毫没有透露他的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 insensible</td><td>/ɪnˈsensəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无知觉的，昏迷的</td><td>insensible to pain<br>感觉不到疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The blow left him insensible for several minutes.  这一击使他几分钟内失去了知觉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accessory</b></td><td>/əkˈsesəri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>辅助的，附属的</td><td>an accessory building<br>一座附属建筑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The garage is an accessory structure behind the main house.  车库是主屋后面的一座附属建筑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peripheral</td><td>/pəˈrɪfərəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>辅助性的，次要的</td><td>of peripheral importance<br>只具次要意义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That issue is peripheral to our main discussion today.  那个问题对我们今天的主要讨论来说是次要的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supplementary</td><td>/ˌsʌpləˈmentəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>补充的，额外的</td><td>supplementary information<br>补充信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher handed out supplementary reading for the weekend.  老师发了周末的补充阅读材料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 of primarily importance</td><td>/əv ˈpraɪmeri ɪmˈpɔːrtəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>最重要的，首要的</td><td>a matter of primary importance<br>头等重要的事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Safety is of primary importance in every laboratory procedure.  在每一项实验室操作中，安全都是最重要的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 chief</td><td>/ˈtʃiːf/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；首领</td><td>the chief reason<br>主要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cost was the chief reason they abandoned the project.  成本是他们放弃这个项目的主要原因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 main</td><td>/ˈmeɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>主要的，最重要的</td><td>the main point<br>主要论点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The main problem is that we have almost no time.  主要问题是我们几乎没有时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 principal</td><td>/ˈprɪnsəpəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>主要的，首要的；校长</td><td>the principal cause<br>首要原因</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourism is the principal source of income on the island.  旅游业是这个岛上的主要收入来源。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accidental</b></td><td>/ˌæksɪˈdentl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>意外发生的，偶然的</td><td>an accidental discovery<br>一个偶然的发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fire was accidental, not the result of arson.  这场火灾是意外，不是纵火所致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incidental</td><td>/ˌɪnsɪˈdentəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶然的；附带的</td><td>incidental expenses<br>杂项开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The damage was incidental to the main purpose of the test.  这种损坏只是测试主要目的之外的附带结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fortuitous</td><td>/fɔːrˈtuːɪtəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶然的，碰巧的</td><td>a fortuitous meeting<br>一次偶然的相遇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their fortuitous meeting at the airport changed both careers.  他们在机场的偶然相遇改变了两人的职业道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unintended</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈtendɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非故意的，无意的</td><td>unintended consequences<br>意外的后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new rule had several unintended consequences for small firms.  新规定对小公司产生了几个意料之外的后果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unintentional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnˈtenʃənəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无意的，非故意的</td><td>an unintentional insult<br>无意的冒犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Any offence caused by my remark was entirely unintentional.  我的话若造成任何冒犯，完全是无意的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unpremeditated</td><td>/ˌʌnpriˈmedɪteɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非预谋的，未事先计划的</td><td>an unpremeditated attack<br>非预谋的袭击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court decided the killing was sudden and unpremeditated.  法庭认定这起杀人是突发且非预谋的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unwitting</td><td>/ʌnˈwɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不知道的，未觉察的</td><td>an unwitting accomplice<br>不知情的帮凶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became an unwitting participant in the whole scheme.  她不知不觉地成了整个阴谋的参与者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calculated</td><td>/ˈkælkjuleɪtɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过深思熟虑的，有意的</td><td>a calculated risk<br>经过权衡的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investing now was a calculated risk that finally paid off.  现在投资是经过盘算的风险，最终有了回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intended</td><td>/ɪnˈtendɪd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预期的，有意的</td><td>the intended audience<br>目标读者群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book never reached its intended audience of young readers.  这本书从未触及它预期的年轻读者群。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 planned</td><td>/ˈplænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>计划好的，有计划的</td><td>a carefully planned trip<br>精心策划的旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole ceremony was planned down to the last detail.  整个典礼被计划到了最后一个细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 premeditated</td><td>/priˈmedəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预谋的，事先策划的</td><td>premeditated murder<br>预谋杀人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors argued that the robbery was carefully premeditated.  检察官认为这起抢劫是精心预谋的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acclimate</b></td><td>/ˈækləmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使适应，使习惯</td><td>acclimate to the altitude<br>适应海拔高度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the climbers a week to acclimate to the altitude.  登山者花了一周时间适应这个海拔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjust</td><td>/əˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>调整；使适应</td><td>adjust to a new job<br>适应新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took her months to adjust to life in a new country.  她花了几个月才适应在新国家的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accommodate</td><td>/əˈkɑːmədeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使适应；容纳；迁就</td><td>accommodate different needs<br>满足不同需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule was changed to accommodate everyone's travel plans.  日程被改动以适应每个人的出行计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shape</td><td>/ˈʃeɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使成形，塑造</td><td>shape public opinion<br>塑造公众舆论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early experiences shape the way children see the world.  早期经历塑造了孩子看世界的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suit</td><td>/ˈsuːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>适合，与……相配</td><td>suit your needs<br>适合你的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We can arrange a time that suits your schedule better.  我们可以安排一个更适合你日程的时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tailor</td><td>/ˈteɪlər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使适合，专门制作</td><td>tailor a course to students<br>为学生定制课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The training is tailored to each employee's actual duties.  培训是根据每位员工的实际职责量身定制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 make unfamiliar with</td><td>/meɪk ˌʌnfəˈmɪljər wɪð/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使不熟悉，使生疏</td><td>make unfamiliar with a place<br>使不熟悉某地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad had made him unfamiliar with his own hometown streets.  多年在国外让他对故乡的街道感到生疏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accolade</b></td><td>/ˈækəleɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>赞扬，荣誉；奖励</td><td>win the highest accolade<br>赢得最高荣誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel received accolades from critics on both continents.  这部小说获得了两大洲评论家的赞誉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 applause</td><td>/əˈplɔːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>鼓掌；认可，赞许</td><td>a round of applause<br>一阵掌声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her speech was greeted with long and warm applause.  她的演讲赢得了长时间热烈的掌声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 credit</td><td>/ˈkredɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>表扬，赞扬；信任</td><td>take credit for<br>把功劳归于自己</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She deserves credit for keeping the project alive.  她值得称赞，因为她让这个项目坚持下来了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinction</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>殊荣，卓越；区别</td><td>a mark of distinction<br>卓越的标志</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He graduated with distinction from a small rural college.  他以优异成绩从一所乡下的小型学院毕业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disapprobation</td><td>/ˌdɪsæprəˈbeɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不赞成，非难</td><td>public disapprobation<br>公众的非难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His remarks drew strong disapprobation from senior colleagues.  他的言论招致资深同事的强烈非难。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 excoriate</td><td>/ɪkˈskɔːrieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>严厉批评；擦伤皮肤</td><td>excoriate the government<br>严厉批评政府</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics excoriated the film for its casual cruelty.  批评家严厉批评该片的随意残忍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 criticise</td><td>/ˈkrɪtɪsaɪz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，指责</td><td>criticise the decision<br>批评该决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several members criticised the plan as far too expensive.  几位成员批评这个计划太过昂贵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reprobate</td><td>/ˈreprəbeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>堕落者，道德败坏的人</td><td>an old reprobate<br>一个老无赖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The novel's hero is a charming reprobate with no money.  小说的主角是个迷人却身无分文的堕落者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 castigate</td><td>/ˈkæstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强烈（公开）指责</td><td>castigate for negligence<br>因疏忽而受斥责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge castigated the company for its negligence.  法官严厉指责该公司的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 derogate</td><td>/ˈderəɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>贬损，诋毁</td><td>derogate from an achievement<br>贬损某项成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing should derogate from her remarkable achievement here.  什么都不应贬损她在这里的非凡成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reproof</td><td>/rɪˈpruːf/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>a gentle reproof<br>温和的责备</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A single glance from her served as a silent reproof.  她的一个眼神就成了无声的责备。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 denigrate</td><td>/ˈdenɪɡreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诋毁，污蔑</td><td>denigrate a rival<br>诋毁对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never denigrates his opponents, even after losing badly.  即使惨败，他也从不诋毁对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 denunciate</del></td><td>/dɪˈnʌnsieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开谴责，指责</td><td>denunciate the regime<br>公开谴责该政权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Speakers denunciated the new law at the crowded rally.  演讲者在拥挤的集会上公开谴责这项新法律。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accommodate</b></td><td>/əˈkɑːmədeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使适应；容纳；迁就</td><td>accommodate different needs<br>满足不同需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule was changed to accommodate everyone's travel plans.  日程被改动以适应每个人的出行计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 acclimate</td><td>/ˈækləmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使适应，使习惯</td><td>acclimate to the altitude<br>适应海拔高度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took the climbers a week to acclimate to the altitude.  登山者花了一周时间适应这个海拔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjust</td><td>/əˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>调整；使适应</td><td>adjust to a new job<br>适应新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took her months to adjust to life in a new country.  她花了几个月才适应在新国家的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 condition</td><td>/kənˈdɪʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使适应，训练；条件</td><td>be conditioned to expect<br>被训练成习惯期待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Athletes are conditioned to endure long periods of pain.  运动员被训练得能长时间承受痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conform</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使协调；遵照，遵从</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All new buildings must conform to strict safety codes.  所有新建筑都必须符合严格的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doctor</td><td>/ˈdɑːktər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>篡改，窜改；医生</td><td>doctor the accounts<br>篡改账目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of doctoring the financial records last year.  他被指控篡改了去年的财务记录。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 edit</td><td>/ˈedɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>编辑，修改</td><td>edit a manuscript<br>编辑手稿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She edited the report to make its tone less aggressive.  她修改了报告，使语气不那么强硬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shape</td><td>/ˈʃeɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使成形，塑造</td><td>shape public opinion<br>塑造公众舆论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early experiences shape the way children see the world.  早期经历塑造了孩子看世界的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suit</td><td>/ˈsuːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>适合，与……相配</td><td>suit your needs<br>适合你的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We can arrange a time that suits your schedule better.  我们可以安排一个更适合你日程的时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tailor</td><td>/ˈteɪlər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使适合，专门制作</td><td>tailor a course to students<br>为学生定制课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The training is tailored to each employee's actual duties.  培训是根据每位员工的实际职责量身定制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attune</td><td>/əˈtuːn/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使协调，使和谐</td><td>attune oneself to<br>使自己适应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good teachers are attuned to the moods of their students.  好老师能敏锐感知学生的情绪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conciliate</td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；调解</td><td>conciliate angry customers<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager tried to conciliate the angry customers with refunds.  经理试图用退款来安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coordinate</td><td>/koʊˈɔːrdɪneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>协调，使配合</td><td>coordinate the relief effort<br>协调救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two agencies coordinated the rescue effort after the flood.  洪水过后两个机构协调了救援工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reconcile</td><td>/ˈrekənsaɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使和解，协调</td><td>reconcile conflicting views<br>调和相互冲突的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hard to reconcile these two versions of the story.  很难把这个故事的两种说法协调一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 disharmonize</del></td><td>/dɪsˈhɑrmənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不协调，使失和</td><td>disharmonize a group<br>使团体失和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Careless remarks can disharmonize an otherwise united team.  不慎的言论会使原本团结的团队失和。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accentuate</b></td><td>/əkˈsentʃueɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调，突出</td><td>accentuate the difference<br>突出差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bright lighting accentuated every flaw in the painting.  明亮的灯光突显出这幅画的每处瑕疵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accent</td><td>/ˈæksent/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，突出；口音</td><td>accent the positive<br>强调积极的一面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new design accents clean lines and natural light.  新设计突出了简洁的线条和自然光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 emphasize</td><td>/ˈemfəˌsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强调，着重</td><td>emphasize the importance of<br>强调……的重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She emphasized that the deadline could not be moved.  她强调截止日期不能改动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreground</td><td>/ˈfɔːrɡraʊnd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，重视</td><td>foreground an issue<br>突出某一问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report foregrounds the needs of rural communities.  这份报告着重强调农村社区的需求。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 highlight</td><td>/ˈhaɪˌlaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>强调，突出</td><td>highlight the main findings<br>强调主要发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The study highlights the risks of sitting all day.  这项研究强调了整天坐着的风险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 de-emphasize</td><td>/diˈɛmfəsaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>不再强调，减轻重视</td><td>de-emphasize test scores<br>淡化考试分数</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy de-emphasizes test scores in admissions decisions.  新政策在录取决定中不再强调考试分数。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 play down</td><td>/pleɪ ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>轻描淡写，淡化</td><td>play down the risks<br>淡化风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials tried to play down the scale of the leak.  官员们试图淡化这次泄漏的规模。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 downplay</td><td>/ˌdaʊnˈpleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>轻描淡写，不予重视</td><td>downplay the significance<br>淡化其重要性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He downplayed his own role in the company's success.  他淡化了自己在公司成功中的作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 5

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accrete</b></td><td>/æˈkriːt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>逐渐增长，附着增大</td><td>accrete over time<br>随时间逐渐增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Layers of sediment accreted slowly along the river mouth.  河口处的沉积层缓慢地逐渐增厚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accumulate</td><td>/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>逐渐增长，积累</td><td>accumulate wealth<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust had accumulated on the shelves during our absence.  我们离开期间架子上积了灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 build up</td><td>/bɪld ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>积聚，逐渐增强</td><td>build up pressure<br>积聚压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic builds up quickly on this road after five o'clock.  五点以后这条路上的车流迅速积聚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pile</td><td>/ˈpaɪl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>堆积，累积；一堆</td><td>pile up quickly<br>迅速堆积起来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unread messages piled up while I was away on holiday.  我休假期间未读消息堆积起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wear away</td><td>/wɛr əˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>磨损，逐渐消耗</td><td>wear away the rock<br>磨蚀岩石</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Centuries of wind and rain slowly wore away the temple carvings.  几百年的风雨慢慢磨蚀了神庙的雕刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>accumulate</b></td><td>/əˈkjuːmjəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>逐渐增长，积累</td><td>accumulate wealth<br>积累财富</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dust had accumulated on the shelves during our absence.  我们离开期间架子上积了灰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mount</td><td>/ˈmaʊnt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、及物动词</td><td>增长，上升</td><td>tension mounts<br>紧张气氛加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pressure on the minister mounted as the scandal spread.  随着丑闻扩散，部长承受的压力不断增加。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 swell</td><td>/ˈswel/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>膨胀，增大</td><td>swell in numbers<br>数量增加</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd swelled to thousands by early evening.  傍晚时人群增加到了数千人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garner</td><td>/ˈɡɑːrnər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>获得，积累</td><td>garner support<br>获得支持</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal garnered support from both major parties.  该提案获得了两大党的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snowball</td><td>/ˈsnoʊˌbɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>迅速增长，滚雪球般扩大</td><td>snowball into a crisis<br>演变成一场危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small complaint snowballed into a national controversy.  一个小小的投诉迅速演变成全国性争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 build up</td><td>/bɪld ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>积聚，逐渐增强</td><td>build up pressure<br>积聚压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic builds up quickly on this road after five o'clock.  五点以后这条路上的车流迅速积聚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bulk</td><td>/ˈbʌlk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>变大，增大；大量</td><td>bulk up<br>增重，长壮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He bulked up considerably after months of weight training.  几个月的力量训练后他体格壮实了许多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissipate</td><td>/ˈdɪsɪpeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>驱散，消散；浪费</td><td>dissipate the fog<br>驱散雾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The morning mist dissipated as soon as the sun rose.  太阳一升起，晨雾就消散了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recede</td><td>/rɪˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退去，消退，减弱</td><td>recede into the distance<br>渐渐远去消失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The floodwaters slowly receded from the village streets.  洪水缓缓从村庄的街道退去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acerbic</b></td><td>/əˈsɜːrbɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（语调）尖酸的，刻薄的</td><td>acerbic wit<br>尖刻的机智</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reviewer is famous for her acerbic comments on new plays.  这位评论家以对新剧的尖酸评论闻名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pungent</td><td>/ˈpʌndʒənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（言辞）辛辣尖刻的；刺鼻的</td><td>pungent criticism<br>辛辣的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pungent criticism of the report left everyone at the table silent.  他对这份报告辛辣的批评让全桌人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sardonic</td><td>/sɑːrˈdɑːnɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>讥讽的，嘲笑的</td><td>a sardonic smile<br>讥讽的微笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered with a sardonic laugh and said nothing more.  他讥讽地笑了一声，什么也没再说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 satiric</td><td>/səˈtɪrɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>讽刺的，讥讽的</td><td>a satiric novel<br>一部讽刺小说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The magazine published a satiric cartoon about the election.  这家杂志刊登了一幅关于选举的讽刺漫画。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scalding</td><td>/ˈskɔːldɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>滚烫的；（言辞）严厉的</td><td>scalding criticism<br>严厉的批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic wrote a scalding review of the opening night.  评论家为首演之夜写了一篇严厉的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scathing</td><td>/ˈskeɪðɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>尖酸刻薄的，严厉的</td><td>a scathing attack<br>尖刻的攻击</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She made a scathing remark about his sloppy research.  她对他草率的研究作了一句尖刻的评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sweet</td><td>/ˈswiːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>甜的；温和的，亲切的</td><td>in a sweet tone<br>以温和的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a sweet voice that calmed the frightened child.  她用温柔的声音说话，安抚了害怕的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 saccharine</td><td>/ˈsækərɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>甜得发腻的；矫情做作的</td><td>saccharine sentimentality<br>矫情做作的伤感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film's ending is far too saccharine for my taste.  这部电影的结局对我来说太过矫情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acme</b></td><td>/ˈækmi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，极点</td><td>the acme of perfection<br>完美的极致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That performance marked the acme of her long career.  那场演出标志着她漫长职业生涯的顶峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 culmination</td><td>/ˌkʌlməˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶点，高潮，最终结果</td><td>the culmination of efforts<br>努力的最终成果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize was the culmination of twenty years of research.  这个奖项是二十年研究的最高成果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pinnacle</td><td>/ˈpɪnəkl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>顶峰，巅峰</td><td>the pinnacle of success<br>成功的顶峰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At thirty she had reached the pinnacle of her profession.  三十岁时她已达到职业生涯的巅峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tip-top</td><td>/ˈtɪp ˈtɑp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>最高点；极好的</td><td>in tip-top condition<br>处于极佳状态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She keeps her old bicycle in tip-top condition all year.  她全年都把旧自行车保持在极佳状态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 high-water mark</td><td>/ˈhaɪ ˌwɔtər ˈmɑrk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>最高点，鼎盛时期</td><td>a new high-water mark<br>新的最高点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That treaty was the high-water mark of postwar cooperation.  那项条约是战后合作的顶峰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 bottom</td><td>/ˈbɑːtəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>底部，最低点</td><td>at the bottom<br>在底部</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their team finished at the bottom of the league again.  他们的球队又一次排在联赛末位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nadir</td><td>/ˈneɪdɪr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>最低点，谷底</td><td>reach the nadir<br>到达最低点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His confidence reached its nadir after the third rejection.  第三次被拒后他的信心降到了最低点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rock bottom</td><td>/ˌrɑk ˈbɑtəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>最低点，谷底</td><td>hit rock bottom<br>跌到谷底</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prices hit rock bottom before the market finally recovered.  价格跌到谷底后市场才终于回升。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acquiesce</b></td><td>/ˌækwiˈes/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>勉强同意；默许</td><td>acquiesce in the decision<br>默许该决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She acquiesced in their plan rather than start an argument.  她默许了他们的计划，以免引起争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assent</td><td>/əˈsent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>assent to the proposal<br>赞同该提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee assented to the proposal without further debate.  委员会未经进一步辩论就同意了该提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consent</td><td>/kənˈsent/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>同意，赞同</td><td>give one's consent<br>表示同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children cannot travel without their parents' consent.  没有父母的同意，孩子们不能出行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 subscribe</td><td>/səbˈskraɪb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>赞同（观点）；订阅</td><td>subscribe to a view<br>赞同某观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I do not subscribe to the view that money buys happiness.  我不赞同金钱能买到幸福的观点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 come round</td><td>/kʌm ˈraʊnd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>改变主意而同意；苏醒</td><td>come round to an idea<br>转而接受某想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took weeks, but she finally came round to our plan.  过了几周，她终于转变态度，同意了我们的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 resist</td><td>/rɪˈzɪst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>抵抗，抗拒，不服从</td><td>resist the temptation<br>抵抗诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers resisted every attempt to move them elsewhere.  村民们抵制一切要迁走他们的企图。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defy</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>蔑视，公然违抗</td><td>defy an order<br>违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of workers defied the ban and gathered in the main square.  数千名工人公然违抗禁令，聚集在中心广场上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dissent</td><td>/dɪˈsent/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>持异议，不同意；反对正统</td><td>voice one's dissent<br>表达异议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two judges dissented from the majority opinion of the court.  两位法官对法院的多数意见持异议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acrid</b></td><td>/ˈækrɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刻薄的；辛辣刺鼻的</td><td>acrid smoke<br>刺鼻的烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An acrid smell of burning plastic filled the hallway.  一股塑料烧焦的刺鼻气味充满了走廊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gentle</td><td>/ˈdʒentəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温和的，轻柔的</td><td>a gentle voice<br>温柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave the door a gentle push and stepped inside.  他轻轻推了一下门，走了进去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acrimonious</b></td><td>/ˌækrɪˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>刻薄的，充满仇恨的</td><td>an acrimonious divorce<br>一场充满怨恨的离婚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The meeting ended in an acrimonious exchange of accusations.  会议在互相指责的激烈争吵中结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 embittered</td><td>/ɪmˈbɪtərd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怨恨的，愤懑的</td><td>an embittered old man<br>一个愤懑的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of failure left him embittered and deeply suspicious.  多年的失败使他满怀怨恨且极度多疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hard</td><td>/ˈhɑːrd/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>严厉的，苛刻的；困难的</td><td>be hard on someone<br>对某人严厉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't be so hard on her; she is still learning.  别对她那么严厉，她还在学。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rancorous</td><td>/ˈræŋkərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>怨恨的，充满恶意的</td><td>a rancorous dispute<br>充满恶意的争执</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two brothers ended up in a rancorous quarrel over the inheritance.  两兄弟最后为遗产爆发了一场充满怨恨的争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 resentful</td><td>/rɪˈzentfəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愤恨的，不满的</td><td>resentful of criticism<br>对批评心怀不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She grew resentful when her contributions were repeatedly ignored.  当她的付出被一再忽视时，她开始心生怨恨。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sore</td><td>/ˈsɔːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恼火的，气愤的；疼痛的</td><td>still sore about it<br>对此仍然恼火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is still sore about losing the election last year.  他对去年落选的事仍然很气恼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acumen</b></td><td>/ˈækjəmən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不同寻常的洞察力和鉴别力</td><td>business acumen<br>商业头脑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her financial acumen turned a small shop into a national chain.  她的商业洞察力把一家小店变成了全国连锁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keenness</td><td>/ˈkiːnnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敏锐，锐利；热切</td><td>keenness of perception<br>敏锐的洞察力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old judge was admired for the keenness of his legal mind.  这位老法官因其敏锐的法律头脑而受人敬佩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shrewdness</td><td>/ˈʃruːdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>精明，敏锐的判断力</td><td>political shrewdness<br>政治上的精明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took real shrewdness to buy the land before prices rose.  在地价上涨之前买下那块地需要真正的精明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 canniness</del></td><td>/ˈkæninəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>精明，机警</td><td>the canniness of a veteran trader<br>老练交易员的精明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His canniness about market timing made him a fortune.  他对入市时机的精明判断让他发了财。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 clear-sightedness</td><td>/ˌklɪr ˈsaɪtɪdnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>有远见，眼光敏锐</td><td>remarkable clear-sightedness<br>非凡的远见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We need the clear-sightedness to admit that the plan has failed.  我们需要有清醒的眼光去承认这个计划已经失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 hardheadedness</del></td><td>/ˌhɑrdˈhɛdɪdnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>务实精明；固执</td><td>practical hardheadedness<br>务实的精明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her hardheadedness about costs saved the project from collapse.  她在成本上的务实精明使项目免于崩溃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unable to discern</td><td>/ʌnˈeɪbəl tu dɪˈsɜrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无法辨别，缺乏洞察力</td><td>unable to discern the difference<br>无法辨别差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without training he was unable to discern the fake from the original.  没有受过训练，他无法辨别赝品与真品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>acute</b></td><td>/əˈkjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的；（程度、影响）极强的</td><td>an acute sense of smell<br>敏锐的嗅觉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Dogs have an acute sense of hearing that far exceeds ours.  狗的听觉极其敏锐，远超人类。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fine</td><td>/ˈfaɪn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>细微的，精细的</td><td>a fine distinction<br>细微的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drew a fine distinction between courage and recklessness.  他在勇敢与莽撞之间划出了细微的区别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 keen</td><td>/ˈkiːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的，锐利的</td><td>a keen eye for detail<br>对细节的敏锐眼光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographer has a keen eye for unusual light.  这位摄影师对特别的光线有敏锐的眼力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perceptive</td><td>/pərˈseptɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏锐的</td><td>a perceptive observation<br>敏锐的观察</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her perceptive comments revealed how carefully she had listened.  她敏锐的评论显示出她听得多么仔细。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dreadful</td><td>/ˈdredfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，糟透的</td><td>a dreadful mistake<br>可怕的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had a dreadful headache and went home early.  她头疼得厉害，很早就回家了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excruciating</td><td>/ɪkˈskruːʃieɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极度痛苦的，难以忍受的</td><td>excruciating pain<br>极度的疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excruciating pain in his back made walking impossible.  他背部剧烈的疼痛使他根本无法行走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 profound</td><td>/proʊˈfaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深刻的，深远的</td><td>a profound influence<br>深远的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her death had a profound effect on the whole family.  她的死对整个家庭产生了深远的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 moderate</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəreɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>缓和，减轻；适度的</td><td>moderate one's demands<br>降低自己的要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to moderate his tone during the debate.  他同意在辩论中缓和语气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soft</td><td>/ˈsɑːft/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>柔和的，轻微的</td><td>a soft voice<br>轻柔的声音</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a soft tone so the baby would not wake.  她轻声说话，以免把婴儿弄醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adamant</b></td><td>/ˈædəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的，不可动摇的</td><td>adamant about the rules<br>对规则毫不通融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was adamant that no player would skip practice.  教练坚决要求没有球员可以缺席训练。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hardheaded</td><td>/ˈhɑːrdˌhedɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；务实精明的</td><td>a hardheaded negotiator<br>强硬的谈判者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her hardheaded refusal to compromise stalled the talks for weeks.  她固执地拒绝妥协，使谈判停滞了数周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headstrong</td><td>/ˈhedˌstrɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>任性的，固执的</td><td>a headstrong child<br>任性的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The headstrong young man ignored every warning from his parents.  这个任性的年轻人对父母的每句劝告都置之不理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intransigent</td><td>/ɪnˈtrænzɪdʒənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，固执的</td><td>an intransigent attitude<br>不妥协的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides remained intransigent, so the negotiations collapsed.  双方都毫不妥协，谈判因此破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pertinacious</del></td><td>/ˌpɜːrtnˈeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>顽固的，固执地坚持的</td><td>pertinacious in her demands<br>固执地坚持要求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pertinacious reporter kept calling until she got an answer.  这位执着的记者不停打电话，直到得到答复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obdurate</td><td>/ˈɑːbdərət/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>固执的；冷酷无情的</td><td>remain obdurate<br>仍然固执不改</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The landlord was obdurate and refused to lower the rent.  房东顽固不化，拒绝降低房租。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncompromising</td><td>/ʌnˈkɑːmprəmaɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，坚定的</td><td>an uncompromising stance<br>毫不妥协的立场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took an uncompromising stand against corruption in the department.  她对部门内的腐败采取了毫不妥协的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 vacillatory</del></td><td>/ˈvæsələˌtɔri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>犹豫不决的，摇摆不定的</td><td>vacillatory leadership<br>摇摆不定的领导</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His vacillatory decisions confused everyone on the team.  他摇摆不定的决策让团队里的每个人都很困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incline to yield</td><td>/ɪnˈklaɪn tu ˈjiːld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>倾向于让步，容易屈从</td><td>incline to yield to pressure<br>倾向于屈从压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Under family pressure he began to incline to yield on the matter.  在家庭压力下，他开始倾向于在这件事上让步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amenable</td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relenting</td><td>/rɪˈlentɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>态度软化的，让步的</td><td>show a relenting attitude<br>表现出软化的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After hours of pleading, his relenting expression gave us hope.  在几小时的哀求之后，他态度软化的表情给了我们希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 yielding</td><td>/ˈjiːldɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，易屈服的</td><td>a yielding personality<br>顺从的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her yielding nature made her an easy target for pushy salesmen.  她顺从的性格使她很容易被强势的推销员盯上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 6

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adapt</b></td><td>/əˈdæpt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使适应；改编，改写</td><td>adapt to a new environment<br>适应新环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Immigrants must adapt to unfamiliar customs and a new language.  移民必须适应陌生的习俗和一门新语言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adjust</td><td>/əˈdʒʌst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>调整；使适应</td><td>adjust to a new job<br>适应新工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took her months to adjust to life in a new country.  她花了几个月才适应在新国家的生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conform</td><td>/kənˈfɔːrm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>使协调；遵照，遵从</td><td>conform to the standard<br>符合标准</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All new buildings must conform to strict safety codes.  所有新建筑都必须符合严格的安全规范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 edit</td><td>/ˈedɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>编辑，修改</td><td>edit a manuscript<br>编辑手稿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She edited the report to make its tone less aggressive.  她修改了报告，使语气不那么强硬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accommodate</td><td>/əˈkɑːmədeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使适应；容纳；迁就</td><td>accommodate different needs<br>满足不同需求</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The schedule was changed to accommodate everyone's travel plans.  日程被改动以适应每个人的出行计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 shape</td><td>/ˈʃeɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使成形，塑造</td><td>shape public opinion<br>塑造公众舆论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early experiences shape the way children see the world.  早期经历塑造了孩子看世界的方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suit</td><td>/ˈsuːt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>适合，与……相配</td><td>suit your needs<br>适合你的需要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We can arrange a time that suits your schedule better.  我们可以安排一个更适合你日程的时间。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tailor</td><td>/ˈteɪlər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使适合，专门制作</td><td>tailor a course to students<br>为学生定制课程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The training is tailored to each employee's actual duties.  培训是根据每位员工的实际职责量身定制的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>addict</b></td><td>/ˈædɪkt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>对某事成瘾者；痴迷者</td><td>a television addict<br>电视迷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My brother is a complete coffee addict and drinks six cups daily.  我弟弟是个十足的咖啡瘾者，每天喝六杯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 devotee</td><td>/ˌdevəˈtiː/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>爱好者，热爱者</td><td>a devotee of opera<br>歌剧爱好者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As a devotee of jazz, he attends every festival in the city.  作为爵士乐迷，他参加城里的每一个音乐节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthusiast</td><td>/ɪnˈθuːziæst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>热衷者，爱好者</td><td>a sports enthusiast<br>体育爱好者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The club is full of cycling enthusiasts who ride every weekend.  俱乐部里全是每周末都骑车的自行车爱好者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fanatic</td><td>/fəˈnætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者</td><td>a fitness fanatic<br>健身狂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My roommate is a fitness fanatic who trains twice a day.  我室友是个健身狂，一天训练两次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 maniac</td><td>/ˈmeɪniæk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>狂热者；疯子</td><td>drive like a maniac<br>开车像疯子一样</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a football maniac who never misses a single match.  他是个足球狂，一场比赛都不落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonfan</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnˈfæn/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>非爱好者，不感兴趣者</td><td>appeal even to nonfans<br>连非爱好者也吸引</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The movie was so good that even nonfans of the genre enjoyed it.  这部电影好到连不喜欢这类片子的人都看得很开心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adhere</b></td><td>/ədˈhɪr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>服从，遵守</td><td>adhere to the rules<br>遵守规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All staff must adhere to the safety procedures without exception.  全体员工必须无例外地遵守安全规程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cling to</td><td>/ˈklɪŋ tu/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>坚持，紧抓不放</td><td>cling to old beliefs<br>固守旧观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He continued to cling to the hope that she would return.  他一直抱着她会回来的希望不放。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hew to</td><td>/ˈhjuː tu/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守，严格遵循</td><td>hew to the party line<br>遵循党的路线</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new director promised to hew to the founder's original vision.  新任主管承诺严格遵循创始人最初的理念。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stand by</td><td>/stænd ˈbaɪ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>支持，坚守</td><td>stand by a promise<br>坚守承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I will stand by my decision no matter what critics say.  不管批评者怎么说，我都坚持我的决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stick to</td><td>/ˈstɪk tu/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>坚持，遵守</td><td>stick to the plan<br>坚持原计划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Let's stick to the original plan and avoid last-minute changes.  我们还是坚持原计划，避免临时改动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comply with</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪ wɪð/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>遵守，服从</td><td>comply with regulations<br>遵守规章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Every factory must comply with the new emission standards.  每家工厂都必须遵守新的排放标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 defy</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>蔑视，公然违抗</td><td>defy an order<br>违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of workers defied the ban and gathered in the main square.  数千名工人公然违抗禁令，聚集在中心广场上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disobey</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈbeɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>不服从，违抗</td><td>disobey an order<br>违抗命令</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soldier was punished for choosing to disobey a direct order.  这名士兵因选择违抗直接命令而受到处罚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rebel against</td><td>/rɪˈbɛl əˈɡɛnst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>反抗，违抗</td><td>rebel against authority<br>反抗权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Teenagers often rebel against the rules their parents set.  青少年常常反抗父母定下的规矩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adjourn</b></td><td>/əˈdʒɜːrn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延期，休会</td><td>adjourn the meeting<br>宣布会议休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge decided to adjourn the trial until Monday morning.  法官决定将审判延至周一上午。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 prorogate</del></td><td>/ˈproʊrəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使休会，延期</td><td>prorogate the assembly<br>使议会休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The governor moved to prorogate the assembly for two months.  州长动议将议会休会两个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prorogue</td><td>/proʊˈroʊɡ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使休会，中止议会会期</td><td>prorogue parliament<br>使议会休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prime minister asked the queen to prorogue parliament.  首相请求女王让议会休会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recess</td><td>/rɪˈses/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>休会，休庭；课间休息</td><td>take a short recess<br>短暂休会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee will recess for lunch and resume at two.  委员会将休会吃午饭，两点复会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suspend</td><td>/səˈspend/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暂停，中止</td><td>suspend the hearing<br>中止听证会</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials had to suspend the search because of the storm.  由于暴风雨，官员们不得不中止搜救。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 convoke</td><td>/kənˈvoʊk/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>召集开会</td><td>convoke a special session<br>召集特别会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The president will convoke an emergency session of the council.  总统将召集理事会举行紧急会议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adjunct</b></td><td>/ˈædʒʌŋkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>附属物，非必须部分</td><td>a useful adjunct to therapy<br>治疗的有益辅助手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Exercise is a valuable adjunct to a healthy diet.  运动是健康饮食的有益辅助。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appendage</td><td>/əˈpendɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>附属物，附加物</td><td>a mere appendage<br>仅仅是个附属物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The small museum is treated as an appendage of the university.  这座小博物馆被当作大学的附属机构。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appliance</td><td>/əˈplaɪəns/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>器具，装置</td><td>a kitchen appliance<br>厨房电器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We bought a new appliance to replace the broken refrigerator.  我们买了一件新电器来替换坏掉的冰箱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 attachment</td><td>/əˈtætʃmənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>附件，附加装置；依恋</td><td>a vacuum cleaner attachment<br>吸尘器配件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The drill comes with an attachment for sanding wood.  这台电钻附带一个打磨木头的配件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 add-on</td><td>/ˈæd ˌɑn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>附加物，附加组件</td><td>an optional add-on<br>可选的附加组件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The camera is sold with an optional add-on for night photography.  这台相机附售一个可选的夜摄附加配件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 essential element</td><td>/ɪˈsɛnʃəl ˈɛləmənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>必要成分，核心要素</td><td>an essential element of success<br>成功的必要要素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trust is an essential element of any lasting partnership.  信任是任何持久合作关系的必要要素。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adlib</b></td><td>/ˌædˈlɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>即兴的</td><td>an adlib remark<br>即兴的一句话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His adlib jokes got a bigger laugh than the script did.  他即兴的玩笑比剧本还要逗人笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 extemporary</del></td><td>/ɪkˈstɛmpəˌrɛri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>即席的，无准备的</td><td>an extemporary speech<br>即席演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an extemporary talk that impressed the whole audience.  她做了一场即兴演讲，令全场听众印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impromptu</td><td>/ɪmˈprɑːmptuː/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>即席的表演；即席的，即兴的</td><td>an impromptu performance<br>即兴表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band gave an impromptu concert in the town square.  乐队在镇广场上举行了一场即兴音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 improvisational</td><td>/ˌɪmprɑvɪˈzeɪʃənəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>即兴的，临时创作的</td><td>improvisational comedy<br>即兴喜剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He teaches an improvisational theater class on Thursday nights.  他在周四晚上教即兴戏剧课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 offhanded</del></td><td>/ˌɔfˈhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>随口的，不经思考的</td><td>an offhanded comment<br>随口的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His offhanded reply suggested he had not thought about it at all.  他随口的回答显示他根本没考虑过这件事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 considered</td><td>/kənˈsɪdərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>经过深思的，慎重的</td><td>a considered opinion<br>深思后的意见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 That is my considered judgment after reading the whole report.  这是我读完整份报告后的慎重判断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 planned</td><td>/ˈplænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>计划好的，有计划的</td><td>a carefully planned trip<br>精心策划的旅行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole ceremony was planned down to the last detail.  整个典礼被计划到了最后一个细节。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 premeditated</td><td>/priˈmedəˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预谋的，事先策划的</td><td>premeditated murder<br>预谋杀人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Prosecutors argued that the robbery was carefully premeditated.  检察官认为这起抢劫是精心预谋的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rehearsed</td><td>/rɪˈhɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>排练过的，事先准备好的</td><td>a carefully rehearsed answer<br>精心准备的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her apology sounded rehearsed rather than sincere.  她的道歉听起来像是排练过的，而不真诚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>admonish</b></td><td>/ədˈmɑːnɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>告诫，警告；责备</td><td>admonish someone for lying<br>因撒谎而责备某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The teacher admonished the boys for shouting in the hallway.  老师责备男孩们在走廊里大喊大叫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 counsel</td><td>/ˈkaʊnsəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>建议，劝告</td><td>counsel patience<br>劝人耐心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His lawyer counseled him to say nothing to the press.  他的律师建议他对媒体什么都别说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chide</td><td>/tʃaɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>责备</td><td>chide someone gently<br>温和地责备某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She chided her son for forgetting his grandmother's birthday.  她责备儿子忘了祖母的生日。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprimand</td><td>/ˈreprəˌmænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>训斥，正式谴责</td><td>reprimand an employee<br>训斥员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer was reprimanded for leaving his post early.  这名警官因提早离岗而受到训斥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reproach</td><td>/rɪˈproʊtʃ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批评，责备</td><td>reproach oneself for<br>因某事自责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She reproached him for never returning her calls.  她责备他从不回她的电话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reprove</td><td>/rɪˈpruːv/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>温和地责备，警告</td><td>reprove a student mildly<br>温和地批评学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach reproved the player for arguing with the referee.  教练责备那名球员与裁判争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tick off</td><td>/ˌtɪk ˈɔf/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>斥责；使恼火</td><td>get ticked off by the boss<br>被老板训斥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager ticked him off for arriving late again.  经理因他又迟到而把他训了一顿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adore</b></td><td>/əˈdɔːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>热爱，极喜爱；崇拜</td><td>absolutely adore someone<br>非常喜爱某人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children adore their new puppy.  孩子们非常喜爱他们的新小狗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fancy</td><td>/ˈfænsi/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>喜爱，想要</td><td>fancy a cup of tea<br>想喝杯茶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do you fancy a walk along the river this evening?  今晚想不想去河边散散步？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relish</td><td>/ˈrelɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>享受，喜爱；乐趣，兴味</td><td>with obvious relish<br>带着明显的享受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He described the meal with obvious relish.  他津津有味地描述那顿饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savor</td><td>/ˈseɪvər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>品味，享受</td><td>savor every moment<br>品味每一刻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She paused to savor the smell of fresh bread.  她停下来细细品味新鲜面包的香味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delight in</td><td>/dɪˈlaɪt ɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>喜爱，以…为乐</td><td>delight in teasing others<br>以逗弄别人为乐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children delight in splashing through every puddle.  孩子们喜欢在每个水坑里踩水玩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rejoice in</td><td>/rɪˈdʒɔɪs ɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>为…感到欣喜</td><td>rejoice in good fortune<br>为好运而欣喜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole village rejoiced in the news of her recovery.  全村人都为她康复的消息感到欣喜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cherish</td><td>/ˈtʃerɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>珍爱，珍视</td><td>cherish a memory<br>珍藏一段回忆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I will always cherish the years we spent together.  我会永远珍视我们共度的那些年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abhor</td><td>/əbˈhɔːr/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>极度厌恶，深恶痛绝</td><td>abhor violence<br>痛恨暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She abhors any form of cruelty to animals.  她极度厌恶任何形式的虐待动物行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 abominate</del></td><td>/əˈbɑːmɪneɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，厌恶</td><td>abominate cruelty<br>憎恶残忍</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He abominated the hypocrisy he saw all around him.  他痛恨周围随处可见的虚伪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 despise</td><td>/dɪˈspaɪz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>鄙视，极其不喜欢</td><td>despise a coward<br>鄙视懦夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She despised him for abandoning his closest friends.  她鄙视他抛弃至交的行为。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 detest</td><td>/dɪˈtest/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>厌恶，憎恨</td><td>detest hypocrisy<br>厌恶虚伪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I detest waiting in long lines on hot days.  我讨厌在炎热的日子里排长队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 execrate</del></td><td>/ˈeksɪkreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>痛恨，咒骂</td><td>execrate a tyrant<br>痛斥暴君</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still execrate the tyrant for his cruelty.  史学家仍因其残暴而痛斥这个暴君。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathe</td><td>/loʊð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>厌恶，讨厌</td><td>loathe the sight of<br>一见就厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loathes the smell of boiled cabbage.  他讨厌煮卷心菜的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>adulate</b></del></td><td>/ˈædʒəleɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>极度谄媚，拍马屁</td><td>adulate a celebrity<br>对名人极力吹捧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reporters adulated the young singer after her first hit.  记者们在这位年轻歌手第一首热门歌后对她大加吹捧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overpraise</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈpreɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>过分赞扬</td><td>overpraise a mediocre film<br>过分赞誉平庸电影</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics tend to overpraise any novel by a famous author.  评论家往往对名作家的任何小说都过分赞扬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 belaud</del></td><td>/bɪˈlɔːd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>大加赞扬，过度称赞</td><td>belaud a patron<br>大肆赞美赞助人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court poets belauded the king in every verse.  宫廷诗人在每一行诗里都对国王大加赞美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soft-soap</td><td>/ˈsɔːft ˌsoʊp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>奉承，讨好</td><td>soft-soap the boss<br>奉承老板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He tried to soft-soap the inspector into overlooking the fault.  他试图奉承检查员，让他放过这个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 butter up</td><td>/ˈbʌtər ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>讨好，奉承</td><td>butter up the teacher<br>讨好老师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spent the whole lunch buttering up her new manager.  她整个午饭时间都在讨好她的新经理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scorn</td><td>/skɔːrn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>轻蔑，鄙视，不屑</td><td>scorn an offer<br>不屑接受提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He scorned the idea of asking his parents for money.  他不屑于向父母要钱这个想法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disdain</td><td>/dɪsˈdeɪn/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>轻视，鄙视</td><td>disdain to answer<br>不屑于回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She disdained the flattery of her ambitious colleagues.  她鄙视那些野心勃勃的同事的奉承。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 vituperate</del></td><td>/vaɪˈtuːpəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>谩骂，责骂</td><td>vituperate an opponent<br>谩骂对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The columnist vituperated the mayor in every weekly article.  这位专栏作家在每周的文章里都痛骂市长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disparage</td><td>/dɪˈspærɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低；轻蔑地说</td><td>disparage someone's efforts<br>贬低某人的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not disparage her work simply because she is young.  不要仅仅因为她年轻就贬低她的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adulterate</b></td><td>/əˈdʌltəreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>掺杂，加入低等成分</td><td>adulterate milk with water<br>在牛奶里掺水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dealer was fined for adulterating olive oil with cheaper fat.  这个商人因在橄榄油中掺入廉价油脂而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alloy</td><td>/ˈæˌlɔɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>合金；使（品质）降低，掺杂</td><td>an alloy of copper and tin<br>铜锡合金</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bronze is an alloy of copper and tin used since ancient times.  青铜是自古就使用的铜锡合金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contaminate</td><td>/kənˈtæmɪneɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，感染</td><td>contaminate the water supply<br>污染供水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Leaking chemicals contaminated the soil around the factory.  泄漏的化学品污染了工厂周围的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pollute</td><td>/pəˈluːt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>污染，弄脏</td><td>pollute the environment<br>污染环境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Factories along the river have polluted the water for decades.  沿河的工厂几十年来一直在污染水体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 taint</td><td>/teɪnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>使（品质）污损</td><td>taint someone's reputation<br>损害某人名誉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scandal tainted his reputation for the rest of his career.  这场丑闻在他余下的职业生涯中都损害着他的名声。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 water down</td><td>/ˌwɔːtər ˈdaʊn/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>掺水稀释；削弱</td><td>water down a proposal<br>弱化一项提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lawmakers watered down the bill to win more votes.  议员们削弱了这项法案的力度以争取更多选票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enrich</td><td>/ɪnˈrɪtʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使丰富，增强</td><td>enrich the soil<br>使土壤肥沃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reading widely will enrich your understanding of other cultures.  广泛阅读会丰富你对其他文化的理解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 7

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>adumbrate</b></del></td><td>/ˈædəmbreɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预示</td><td>adumbrate future trouble<br>预示日后的麻烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The early chapters adumbrate the tragedy that follows.  开头几章预示了随后的悲剧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 forerun</del></td><td>/fɔːrˈrʌn/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预示，先于…而来</td><td>forerun a change<br>预示一场变革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The falling leaves forerun the arrival of a long winter.  落叶预示着漫长冬天的到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harbinger</td><td>/ˈhɑːrbɪndʒər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>预兆，先兆；预示者</td><td>a harbinger of spring<br>春天的先兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first robin is often seen as a harbinger of spring.  第一只知更鸟常被视为春天的预兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 herald</td><td>/ˈherəld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>预示，预兆</td><td>herald a new era<br>预示新时代来临</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invention heralded a new era in communication.  这项发明预示着通信新时代的到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prefigure</td><td>/priːˈfɪɡjər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预示，预先显示</td><td>prefigure later events<br>预示后来的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His early sketches prefigure the style of his mature paintings.  他早期的素描预示了他成熟画作的风格。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adventitious</b></td><td>/ˌædvenˈtɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，后天的，非内在的</td><td>an adventitious circumstance<br>偶然的外部情况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her success was not adventitious but the result of years of work.  她的成功不是偶然外来的，而是多年努力的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alien</td><td>/ˈeɪliən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>外来的，陌生的</td><td>alien to our culture<br>与我们的文化格格不入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Such customs seemed completely alien to the newcomers.  这些习俗对新来者来说完全陌生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extraneous</td><td>/ɪkˈstreɪniəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无关的，不重要的</td><td>extraneous information<br>无关的信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please delete all extraneous details from the summary.  请把摘要中所有无关的细节删掉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 external</td><td>/ɪkˈstɜːrnəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外部的，外来的</td><td>external factors<br>外部因素</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was caused by external factors beyond our control.  延误是由我们无法控制的外部因素造成的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreign</td><td>/ˈfɔːrən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>外来的，异质的；外国的</td><td>a foreign object<br>异物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon removed a foreign object from the patient's eye.  外科医生从病人眼中取出一个异物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 supervenient</del></td><td>/ˌsuːpərˈviːniənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>后来附加的，额外发生的</td><td>a supervenient condition<br>后来附加的条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A supervenient illness complicated his already slow recovery.  一场后来出现的疾病使他本已缓慢的康复更加复杂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 constitutional</td><td>/ˌkɑːnstəˈtuːʃənəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>体质上的，内在固有的</td><td>a constitutional weakness<br>体质上的虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His shyness seems constitutional rather than a habit he learned.  他的害羞似乎是天性使然，而非后天习得的习惯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 essential</td><td>/ɪˈsenʃəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>本质的，必不可少的</td><td>an essential quality<br>本质的特性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Patience is an essential part of her character.  耐心是她性格中本质的一部分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intrinsic</td><td>/ɪnˈtrɪnsɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>固有的，内在的</td><td>intrinsic value<br>内在价值</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The painting has an intrinsic beauty independent of its price.  这幅画有着与价格无关的内在美。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inborn</td><td>/ˈɪnˌbɔːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，先天的</td><td>an inborn talent<br>天生的才能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She seems to have an inborn sense of rhythm.  她似乎天生就有节奏感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inbred</td><td>/ˈɪnˌbred/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，根深蒂固的</td><td>an inbred sense of duty<br>天生的责任感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has an inbred distrust of large institutions.  他对大型机构有一种根深蒂固的不信任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 innate</td><td>/ɪˈneɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>天生的，固有的</td><td>an innate ability<br>天生的能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Children have an innate curiosity about the world.  儿童对世界有一种天生的好奇心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inherent</td><td>/ɪnˈhɪrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内在的，本质的</td><td>an inherent risk<br>固有的风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is an inherent danger in climbing without a rope.  无绳攀岩存在内在的危险。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>adversary</b></td><td>/ˈædvərseri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌手，对手</td><td>a formidable adversary<br>强劲的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He respected his adversary even while fighting to win.  即使在拼命求胜时，他也尊重自己的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antagonist</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，敌人</td><td>a bitter antagonist<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her chief antagonist in the debate was a former professor.  她在辩论中的主要对手是一位前教授。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foe</td><td>/ˈfoʊ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌人，仇敌</td><td>friend or foe<br>是友还是敌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the dark he could not tell friend from foe.  在黑暗中他分不清敌友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opponent</td><td>/əˈpoʊnənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，反对者</td><td>a worthy opponent<br>值得尊敬的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She defeated a much stronger opponent in the final round.  她在决赛中击败了一位强得多的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ally</td><td>/ˈælaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>同盟者，支持者；结盟</td><td>a close ally<br>亲密的盟友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Britain remained a close ally of the United States.  英国一直是美国的亲密盟友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amigo</td><td>/əˈmiːˌɡoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>朋友，伙伴</td><td>hey, amigo<br>嘿，朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thanks for the ride, amigo, I owe you one.  谢谢你载我一程，老兄，我欠你一次。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 friend</td><td>/ˈfrend/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>朋友，友人</td><td>a close friend<br>亲密的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has been my closest friend since primary school.  她从小学起就是我最亲密的朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>advert</b></td><td>/ədˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>注意，留意；提及</td><td>advert to a problem<br>提到一个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker briefly adverted to the funding shortage.  演讲者简短地提到了资金短缺问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inattentive</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈtentɪv/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，漫不经心的</td><td>an inattentive student<br>不专心的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inattentive driver failed to see the red light.  这位漫不经心的司机没看到红灯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 remiss</td><td>/rɪˈmɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽的，不留心的</td><td>be remiss in one's duties<br>疏于职守</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 I would be remiss if I did not thank the volunteers.  如果我不感谢志愿者，那就是我的疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 heedless</td><td>/ˈhiːdləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不注意的，不顾的</td><td>heedless of danger<br>不顾危险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ran into the street heedless of the traffic.  他不顾车流冲进了街道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negligent</td><td>/ˈneɡlɪdʒənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>疏忽大意的</td><td>negligent in his duties<br>对职责疏忽大意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company was found negligent in maintaining its equipment.  该公司被认定在设备维护上存在疏忽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>advocate</b></td><td>/ˈædvəkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，提倡</td><td>advocate reform<br>提倡改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many doctors advocate walking as the simplest form of exercise.  许多医生提倡把走路作为最简单的运动方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 back</td><td>/ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>支持，资助</td><td>back a candidate<br>支持某位候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several investors agreed to back her new company.  几位投资人同意资助她的新公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 champion</td><td>/ˈtʃæmpiən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持</td><td>champion a cause<br>支持一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has championed the rights of migrant workers for years.  她多年来一直为农民工的权益奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 endorse</td><td>/ɪnˈdɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>公开支持</td><td>endorse a candidate<br>公开支持某候选人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union voted to endorse the mayor for a second term.  工会投票支持市长连任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 patronize</td><td>/ˈpeɪtrənaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赞助；以高人一等的态度对待</td><td>patronize the arts<br>赞助艺术</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wealthy merchants patronized young painters throughout the Renaissance.  富商在整个文艺复兴时期都赞助年轻画家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impugn</td><td>/ɪmˈpjuːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抨击，质疑（诚信、动机）</td><td>impugn someone's motives<br>质疑某人的动机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He never impugned the honesty of his opponents.  他从不质疑对手的诚实。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affable</b></td><td>/ˈæfəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼的，温和的</td><td>an affable host<br>和蔼的主人</td></tr>
