<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our affable neighbor greets everyone on the street by name.  我们和蔼的邻居在街上会叫出每个人的名字打招呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cordial</td><td>/ˈkɔːrdʒəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情友好的；令人振奋的</td><td>a cordial welcome<br>热情的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two leaders exchanged cordial greetings before the talks.  两位领导人在会谈前互致亲切的问候。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hospitable</td><td>/ˈhɑːspɪtəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情好客的；宜于生长的</td><td>hospitable to strangers<br>对陌生人热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The villagers were remarkably hospitable to the exhausted travelers.  村民对疲惫的旅人非常好客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sociable</td><td>/ˈsoʊʃəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好交际的，友善的</td><td>a sociable personality<br>好交际的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is far more sociable than her quiet older brother.  她比她安静的哥哥爱交际得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 good-natured</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈneɪtʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气好的，和善的</td><td>good-natured teasing<br>善意的调侃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He accepted the joke with a good-natured shrug.  他和善地耸耸肩接受了这个玩笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 good-tempered</td><td>/ˌɡʊd ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>性情温和的，不易发怒的</td><td>a good-tempered child<br>脾气温和的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old dog was good-tempered even when children pulled its ears.  即使孩子们拉它的耳朵，这只老狗也很温顺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-disposed</td><td>/ˌwɛl dɪˈspoʊzd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友善的，有好感的</td><td>well-disposed toward the plan<br>对该计划持赞成态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee seemed well-disposed toward our funding request.  委员会似乎对我们的资金申请持友善态度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irascible</td><td>/ɪˈræsəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>an irascible old man<br>脾气暴躁的老人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The irascible coach shouted at the referee over every call.  这位暴躁的教练对每一次判罚都冲裁判大喊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 testy</td><td>/ˈtesti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，暴躁的</td><td>a testy reply<br>不耐烦的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew testy when the reporters kept repeating the question.  记者反复问同一个问题时，他变得不耐烦起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-tempered</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，易怒的</td><td>an ill-tempered remark<br>一句脾气暴躁的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ill-tempered remark silenced everyone at the dinner table.  他脾气暴躁的话让餐桌上所有人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unamiable</del></td><td>/ʌnˈeɪmiəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友善的，不和气的</td><td>an unamiable manner<br>不友善的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unamiable silence made the other guests feel quite uncomfortable.  他不友善的沉默让其他客人感到很不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ungenial</del></td><td>/ʌnˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友善的；不宜人的</td><td>an ungenial climate<br>不宜人的气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ungenial landlord refused even to greet his tenants.  这位不和气的房东连招呼都不愿跟房客打。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affinity</b></td><td>/əˈfɪnəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喜好，倾向；相似，密切关系</td><td>an affinity for music<br>对音乐的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a natural affinity for languages and picks them up quickly.  她天生对语言有亲近感，学得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bent</td><td>/ˈbent/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>爱好，天生的倾向</td><td>a bent for numbers<br>对数字的天赋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From childhood he showed a bent for mechanical things.  他从小就显示出对机械的爱好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penchant</td><td>/ˈpentʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>嗜好，强烈爱好</td><td>a penchant for luxury<br>对奢华的偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her penchant for dramatic hats made her instantly recognizable.  她对夸张帽子的偏爱让人一眼就认出她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predilection</td><td>/ˌpredəˈlekʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱，偏好</td><td>a predilection for jazz<br>对爵士乐的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic's predilection for older films colored every review.  这位评论家对老电影的偏爱影响了他的每篇评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predisposition</td><td>/ˌpriːdɪspəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，易感性</td><td>a genetic predisposition to<br>遗传易感性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some patients have a predisposition to high blood pressure.  有些病人有高血压的易感倾向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proclivity</td><td>/prəˈklɪvəti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>癖性，偏好（多指不良倾向）</td><td>criminal proclivities<br>犯罪倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proclivity for gambling eventually ruined the family.  他的赌博癖好最终毁了这个家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propensity</td><td>/prəˈpensəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，癖好</td><td>a propensity to exaggerate<br>夸大的倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy has a propensity to lose every umbrella he owns.  这男孩总有把伞丢掉的习性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 association</td><td>/əˌsoʊsiˈeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>联系，关联；协会</td><td>a close association with<br>与…关系密切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He denied any association with the group behind the protest.  他否认与抗议背后的团体有任何关联。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 kinship</td><td>/ˈkɪnˌʃɪp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>亲缘关系；相似，密切联系</td><td>a sense of kinship<br>一种亲近感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt an instant kinship with the other new students.  她立刻对其他新生产生了亲近感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aversion</td><td>/əˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>a strong aversion to<br>对…强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a lifelong aversion to crowded parties.  他一生都厌恶拥挤的聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repugnance</td><td>/rɪˈpʌɡnəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，憎恶</td><td>repugnance at the idea<br>对这想法感到厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could not hide her repugnance at the smell of the fish market.  她无法掩饰对鱼市气味的厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antipathy</td><td>/ænˈtɪpəθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>mutual antipathy<br>相互反感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has long been an antipathy between the two departments.  这两个部门之间长期存在反感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>affluent</b></td><td>/ˈæfluənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的，丰富的</td><td>an affluent suburb<br>富裕的郊区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They moved to a more affluent neighborhood after the promotion.  升职后他们搬到了更富裕的街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 opulent</td><td>/ˈɑːpjələnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的；华丽的</td><td>an opulent hotel lobby<br>豪华的酒店大厅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The opulent ballroom was lined with gold mirrors and velvet chairs.  这间华丽的舞厅摆满了金框镜子和天鹅绒椅。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 loaded</td><td>/ˈloʊdɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（口语）非常有钱的；装满的</td><td>he's absolutely loaded<br>他非常有钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't worry about the bill; her family is loaded.  别担心账单，她家很有钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deep-pocketed</td><td>/ˌdiːp ˈpɑːkɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>财力雄厚的</td><td>deep-pocketed investors<br>财力雄厚的投资者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The startup was rescued by a deep-pocketed backer from abroad.  这家初创公司被一位来自国外的财力雄厚的支持者救了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 silk-stocking</td><td>/ˌsɪlk ˈstɑːkɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>富有的，贵族气派的</td><td>a silk-stocking district<br>富人区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidate raised money mainly in silk-stocking neighborhoods.  这位候选人主要在富人区筹款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-endowed</td><td>/ˌwɛl ɪnˈdaʊd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>资金充足的，天赋优厚的</td><td>a well-endowed university<br>资金充裕的大学</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The well-endowed college offers generous scholarships every year.  这所资金充裕的学院每年提供丰厚奖学金。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-off</td><td>/ˌwɛl ˈɔːf/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的，境况好的</td><td>a well-off family<br>富裕的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her parents were well-off enough to pay her tuition outright.  她父母相当富裕，能一次付清学费。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 well-to-do</td><td>/ˌwɛl tə ˈduː/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>富裕的，小康的</td><td>a well-to-do merchant<br>富裕的商人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school mostly serves children from well-to-do families.  这所学校主要招收富裕家庭的孩子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 needy</td><td>/ˈniːdi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；情感上过度依赖的</td><td>needy families<br>贫困家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity distributes winter coats to needy children.  这家慈善机构向贫困儿童发放冬衣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impecunious</td><td>/ˌɪmpɪˈkjuːniəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫穷的，没钱的</td><td>an impecunious student<br>身无分文的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As an impecunious young painter, he lived on bread and tea.  作为一个穷困的年轻画家，他靠面包和茶为生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impoverished</td><td>/ɪmˈpɑːvərɪʃt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；贫瘠的</td><td>an impoverished region<br>贫困地区</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 War left the once-prosperous city utterly impoverished for many years.  战争让这座曾繁荣的城市多年极度贫困。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indigent</td><td>/ˈɪndɪdʒənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫穷的，贫困的</td><td>indigent defendants<br>贫困的被告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic provides free care to indigent patients.  这家诊所为贫困病人提供免费医疗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 penurious</del></td><td>/pəˈnʊriəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>贫困的；吝啬的</td><td>penurious circumstances<br>贫困的境况</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent a penurious youth in a crowded tenement.  他在拥挤的廉租房里度过了贫困的青年时期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aggrandize</b></td><td>/əˈɡrændaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>扩大（权力、财富、声望）</td><td>aggrandize one's power<br>扩张自己的权力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor used the crisis to aggrandize his own power.  市长利用危机扩大自己的权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 augment</td><td>/ɔɡˈment/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增加，增强</td><td>augment one's income<br>增加收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She augments her salary by teaching evening classes.  她通过教夜校课程增加收入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boost</td><td>/ˈbuːst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>提高，促进；推动</td><td>boost sales<br>促进销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tax cut is expected to boost consumer spending.  减税预计会促进消费者支出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 magnify</td><td>/ˈmæɡnəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>放大；夸大</td><td>magnify the problem<br>夸大问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lens magnifies the specimen four hundred times.  这个镜头把样本放大四百倍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 add</td><td>/ˈæd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>增加，添加；补充说</td><td>add to the confusion<br>加剧混乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The heavy rain only added to our travel troubles.  大雨只是加重了我们的旅行麻烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relegate</td><td>/ˈreləˌɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降级；把…归类</td><td>relegate to the basement<br>把…扔到地下室</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After the scandal he was relegated to a minor post.  丑闻之后他被贬到一个次要职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disparage</td><td>/dɪˈspærɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低；轻蔑地说</td><td>disparage someone's efforts<br>贬低某人的努力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Do not disparage her work simply because she is young.  不要仅仅因为她年轻就贬低她的工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 efface</td><td>/ɪˈfeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擦掉，抹去；使不显眼</td><td>efface all traces<br>抹去所有痕迹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Time had effaced the inscription on the old gravestone.  时间已把这块老墓碑上的铭文磨去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abase</td><td>/əˈbeɪs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>降低（地位、威望、尊严）</td><td>abase oneself before someone<br>在某人面前自贬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would not abase himself by begging for the job.  他不愿低声下气地乞求这份工作。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 demean</td><td>/dɪˈmiːn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>贬低，降低尊严</td><td>demean oneself<br>自贬身份</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He refused to demean his colleagues in front of clients.  他拒绝在客户面前贬低同事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aggravate</b></td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complicate</td><td>/ˈkɑːmplɪkeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使复杂化</td><td>complicate matters further<br>使事情更复杂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New evidence complicated the investigation and delayed the final report.  新证据使调查复杂化，并推迟了最终报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succor</td><td>/ˈsʌkər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>救援，援助</td><td>bring succor to the wounded<br>救助伤员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers hurried to succor the families left homeless by the flood.  志愿者们赶去救助因洪水而无家可归的家庭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 console</td><td>/kənˈsoʊl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，慰藉</td><td>console a grieving friend<br>安慰悲伤的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing anyone said could console her that evening.  那天晚上无论谁说什么都无法安慰她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aggregate</b></td><td>/ˈæɡrəɡət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词、动词</td><td>总数，合计；聚集的</td><td>in the aggregate<br>总的来说，总计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aggregate of small donations exceeded a million dollars.  小额捐款的总额超过了一百万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sum</td><td>/sʌm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>总和，金额；概括</td><td>a large sum of money<br>一大笔钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sum of the three numbers is exactly one hundred.  这三个数的总和正好是一百。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 summation</td><td>/səˈmeɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>总和；总结，概述</td><td>a brief summation<br>简短的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lawyer's summation lasted nearly an hour and convinced the jury.  律师的总结陈述持续了近一小时，说服了陪审团。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 totality</td><td>/toʊˈtæləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>全部，总体</td><td>the totality of the evidence<br>全部证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Judged in its totality, the project was a success.  从整体来看，这个项目是成功的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 coalesce</td><td>/ˌkoʊəˈles/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，融合</td><td>coalesce into a group<br>合并成一个团体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scattered protests coalesced into a national movement.  分散的抗议汇合成一场全国性运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 join together</td><td>/dʒɔɪn təˈɡɛðər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>结合起来，联合在一起</td><td>join together in protest<br>联合起来抗议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several small farms joined together to sell their produce.  几个小农场联合起来出售农产品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 isolated units</td><td>/ˈaɪsəˌleɪtɪd ˈjuːnɪts/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>孤立的个体，分散的单元</td><td>remain isolated units<br>仍是孤立的个体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without a shared plan, the teams stayed isolated units.  缺少共同计划，各团队仍是孤立的单元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aggressive</b></td><td>/əˈɡresɪv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的；积极进取的，强有力的</td><td>an aggressive marketing campaign<br>强势的营销活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog became aggressive whenever strangers approached the gate.  只要有陌生人靠近大门，这只狗就变得有攻击性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fierce</td><td>/ˈfɪrs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凶猛的；激烈的</td><td>fierce competition<br>激烈的竞争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two teams faced fierce competition for the final spot.  两队为最后一个名额展开激烈竞争。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assaultive</td><td>/əˈsɔːltɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好攻击的，暴力的</td><td>assaultive behavior<br>暴力攻击行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient's assaultive behavior required constant supervision by trained staff.  这名病人的攻击性行为需要受过训练的人员持续看护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 combative</td><td>/kəmˈbætɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，好争辩的</td><td>a combative tone<br>好斗的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He answered every question in a combative tone.  他用好斗的语气回答每一个问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 militant</td><td>/ˈmɪlətənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>激进好战的；激进分子</td><td>a militant activist<br>激进的活动人士</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union took a militant stance in the pay talks.  工会在薪资谈判中采取了强硬立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 go-getting</td><td>/ˈɡoʊ ˌɡɛtɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>积极进取的，有闯劲的</td><td>a go-getting young executive<br>有闯劲的年轻主管</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The firm hired a go-getting young manager to revive sales.  公司聘请了一位有闯劲的年轻经理来振兴销售。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 self-assertive</td><td>/ˌsɛlf əˈsɜːrtɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚持自我主张的，自信果断的</td><td>a self-assertive manner<br>自信果断的作风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She learned to be more self-assertive in meetings.  她学会了在会议上更敢于表达主张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 truculent</td><td>/ˈtrʌkjələnt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好战的，好斗的；粗野的</td><td>a truculent response<br>咄咄逼人的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The truculent teenager argued with every teacher who tried to correct him.  这个好斗的少年跟每位试图纠正他的老师都吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pugnacious</td><td>/pʌɡˈneɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好斗的，爱争吵的</td><td>a pugnacious debater<br>好争辩的辩手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pugnacious little dog barked at every passing car.  这只好斗的小狗对每辆过路的车都叫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dynamic</td><td>/daɪˈnæmɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有活力的，动态的</td><td>a dynamic leader<br>有活力的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city has a dynamic arts scene that keeps changing.  这座城市有着不断变化的活跃艺术圈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energetic</td><td>/ˌenərˈdʒetɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力充沛的</td><td>an energetic performance<br>活力四射的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Even at seventy she remains remarkably energetic and full of plans.  即使七十岁了，她依然精力充沛，计划满满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 full-blooded</td><td>/ˌfʊl ˈblʌdɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>强有力的，充满活力的；纯种的</td><td>a full-blooded attack<br>全力以赴的进攻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team launched a full-blooded attack in the second half.  球队在下半场发起了猛烈进攻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vigorous</td><td>/ˈvɪɡərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>精力旺盛的，强有力的</td><td>vigorous exercise<br>剧烈运动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors recommend thirty minutes of vigorous exercise daily.  医生建议每天进行三十分钟的高强度运动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 even-tempered</td><td>/ˌiːvən ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>性情平和的，不易发怒的</td><td>an even-tempered colleague<br>性情平和的同事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed even-tempered throughout the long and difficult negotiation.  在漫长艰难的谈判中他始终保持平和。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonbelligerent</del></td><td>/ˌnɑːnbəˈlɪdʒərənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>非交战的，不好战的</td><td>a nonbelligerent nation<br>非交战国</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty guaranteed the island's nonbelligerent status during the whole conflict.  条约保证了该岛在整场冲突期间的非交战国地位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pacific</td><td>/pəˈsɪfɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>平和的，爱好和平的</td><td>a pacific temperament<br>平和的性情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He settled the quarrel with a few pacific words.  他用几句平和的话平息了争吵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uncombative</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkəmˈbætɪv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不好斗的，不爱争的</td><td>an uncombative reply<br>不带火气的回复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her uncombative answer surprised the hostile interviewer and calmed the room.  她不带火气的回答让不友好的采访者意外，也让场面缓和下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uncontentious</del></td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈtɛnʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不引起争议的，不好争辩的</td><td>an uncontentious proposal<br>无争议的提案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee began with the uncontentious items on the agenda.  委员会从议程上无争议的项目开始。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>aggrieve</b></del></td><td>/əˈɡriːv/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使苦恼，使悲痛；侵害</td><td>the aggrieved party<br>受害方，受损一方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ruling aggrieved thousands of former employees of the closed factory.  这项裁决使那家关闭工厂的数千名前员工感到受了委屈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discontent</td><td>/ˌdɪskənˈtent/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>不满，不满意</td><td>widespread discontent<br>普遍的不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising prices caused growing discontent among the workers.  物价上涨引起工人越来越大的不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disgruntled</td><td>/dɪsˈɡrʌntld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不满的，不高兴的</td><td>a disgruntled customer<br>心怀不满的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A disgruntled employee leaked the memo to the press.  一名心怀不满的员工把备忘录泄露给了媒体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 displeased</td><td>/dɪsˈpliːzd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不高兴的，不满的</td><td>displeased with the result<br>对结果不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director was clearly displeased with the rehearsal.  导演显然对排练不满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dissatisfied</td><td>/dɪˈsætəsˌfaɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不满意的</td><td>dissatisfied with the service<br>对服务不满意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many readers were dissatisfied with the book's ending.  许多读者对这本书的结局不满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 malcontent</td><td>/ˌmælkənˈtent/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>不满分子；不满的</td><td>a group of malcontents<br>一群不满分子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general dismissed the critics as a few malcontents.  将军把批评者说成是几个不满分子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gratify</td><td>/ˈɡrætɪfaɪ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使满足，使高兴</td><td>gratify a desire<br>满足某种欲望</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It gratified her to see her students succeed.  看到学生成功让她很欣慰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>agitate</b></td><td>/ˈædʒɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>煽动，激起；使不安</td><td>agitate for reform<br>鼓动改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Students agitated for changes to the exam system.  学生们为改革考试制度而奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debate</td><td>/dɪˈbeɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>辩论，争论；仔细考虑</td><td>a heated debate<br>激烈的辩论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council debated the housing plan for three hours.  议会就住房计划辩论了三个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dispute</td><td>/dɪˈspjuːt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>争论，争端；对…提出质疑</td><td>settle a dispute<br>解决争端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The neighbors are still in dispute over the fence.  邻居们仍在为那道围栏争执。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bat</td><td>/ˈbæt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>反复讨论；球棒，击球</td><td>bat an idea around<br>反复讨论一个想法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We batted the proposal around for an hour without deciding.  我们把这个提案反复讨论了一小时也没决定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bother</td><td>/ˈbɑːðər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>打扰，使烦恼；费心</td><td>don't bother me<br>别烦我</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The constant noise from the street really bothers me.  街上不停的噪音真让我烦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discomfort</td><td>/dɪsˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>不适，不安；使不舒服</td><td>cause great discomfort<br>引起极大不适</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He shifted in his seat with obvious discomfort.  他明显不自在地在座位上挪动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 discompose</del></td><td>/ˌdɪskəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安；使混乱</td><td>discompose the audience<br>让观众不安</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Nothing seemed to discompose the veteran pilot, not even engine failure.  似乎没什么能让这位老飞行员心慌，连引擎故障也不行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perturb</td><td>/pərˈtɜːrb/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不安，使烦扰</td><td>perturb the balance<br>扰乱平衡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden change of plan perturbed everyone on the team.  计划的突然改变让全队的人都心神不宁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 calm</td><td>/ˈkɑːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、动词、名词</td><td>平静的；使镇静</td><td>calm down quickly<br>很快平静下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a calm voice until the children settled.  她用平静的声音说话，直到孩子们安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 compose</td><td>/kəmˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使镇定；组成；创作</td><td>compose oneself<br>让自己镇定下来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He took a breath to compose himself before speaking.  他深吸一口气让自己镇定下来才开口。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tranquilize</td><td>/ˈtræŋkwəˌlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>使平静，给…服镇静剂</td><td>tranquilize the animal<br>给动物注射镇静剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The vet had to tranquilize the frightened horse.  兽医不得不给这匹惊慌的马注射镇静剂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>agog</b></td><td>/əˈɡɑːɡ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极度感兴趣的，兴奋期待的</td><td>all agog with excitement<br>兴奋不已，满心期待</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were agog at the sight of the elephants.  孩子们看到大象时兴奋不已。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avid</td><td>/ˈævɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>急切渴望的，热衷的</td><td>an avid reader<br>热衷阅读的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My grandmother is an avid gardener even in winter.  我祖母即使在冬天也是个热衷园艺的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ardent</td><td>/ˈɑːrdənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热切的</td><td>an ardent supporter<br>热情的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He remained an ardent fan of the team for fifty years.  他五十年来一直是这支球队的狂热球迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enthusiastic</td><td>/ɪnˌθuːziˈæstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>热情的，热心的</td><td>enthusiastic about the plan<br>对该计划很热心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The students were enthusiastic about the field trip.  学生们对这次实地考察很热情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solicitous</td><td>/səˈlɪsɪtəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>为他人操心的，关切的</td><td>solicitous about her health<br>关心她的健康</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The nurse was solicitous about every detail of his recovery.  护士对他康复的每个细节都很关心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 voracious</td><td>/vəˈreɪʃəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>贪吃的；如饥似渴的</td><td>a voracious appetite<br>极大的食欲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is a voracious reader who finishes a novel each week.  他是个如饥似渴的读者，每周读完一本小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 apathetic</td><td>/ˌæpəˈθetɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠的，无兴趣的</td><td>apathetic voters<br>冷漠的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most students seemed apathetic about the election and did not vote.  大多数学生对这次选举漠不关心，也没有投票。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indifferent</td><td>/ɪnˈdɪfrənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不感兴趣的，冷漠的；平庸的</td><td>indifferent to criticism<br>对批评漠不关心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is entirely indifferent to what her critics say.  她对批评者说什么完全无所谓。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 uneager</del></td><td>/ʌnˈiːɡər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热切的，不情愿的</td><td>uneager to volunteer<br>不愿主动报名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team was oddly uneager to take on the new project.  团队对接手新项目出奇地不积极。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unenthusiastic</td><td>/ˌʌnɪnθuːziˈæstɪk/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不热情的，兴趣不大的</td><td>an unenthusiastic response<br>反应冷淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unenthusiastic nod told us he disliked the idea.  他不情不愿的点头说明他不喜欢这个主意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>agonize</b></td><td>/ˈæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）非常痛苦，苦苦思索</td><td>agonize over a decision<br>为决定苦苦纠结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She agonized for weeks over whether to accept the job.  她为是否接受这份工作苦恼了好几周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anguish</td><td>/ˈæŋɡwɪʃ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>极度痛苦，苦恼</td><td>in great anguish<br>极度痛苦之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His face showed the anguish of a sleepless night.  他的脸上写着一夜无眠的痛苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suffer</td><td>/ˈsʌfər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>受苦，遭受；容忍</td><td>suffer from a headache<br>受头痛折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands suffered from the drought that struck the region that summer.  那年夏天，数千人饱受该地区旱灾之苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>airtight</b></td><td>/ˈertaɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无瑕疵的；密不透气的</td><td>an airtight alibi<br>无懈可击的不在场证明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prosecutor claimed the case against him was airtight.  检察官称对他的指控无懈可击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>alacrity</b></td><td>/əˈlækrəti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>欣然，反应迅速</td><td>accept with alacrity<br>欣然接受</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He agreed to help with surprising alacrity and arrived within minutes.  他出人意料地爽快答应帮忙，几分钟内就到了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 amenability</del></td><td>/əˌmiːnəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>顺从，乐意配合</td><td>amenability to change<br>乐于接受改变</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her amenability to new methods impressed the trainers.  她对新方法的乐于接受让培训师印象深刻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 gameness</del></td><td>/ˈɡeɪmnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>勇于尝试，敢作敢为</td><td>show real gameness<br>表现出真正的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rookie's gameness won over the whole locker room.  这名新人的敢闯劲头赢得了全队的认可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 obligingness</del></td><td>/əˈblaɪdʒɪŋnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>乐于助人，殷勤</td><td>the obligingness of the staff<br>员工的热心周到</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The obligingness of the hotel staff made our stay easy.  酒店员工的热心让我们住得很轻松。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 willingness</td><td>/ˈwɪlɪŋnəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>愿意，乐意</td><td>willingness to help<br>乐意帮忙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her willingness to listen made her a good manager.  她愿意倾听的态度让她成为一名好经理。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 dilatoriness</del></td><td>/ˈdɪlətɔːrinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>拖延，迟缓</td><td>the dilatoriness of the court<br>法院的拖延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee's dilatoriness delayed the repairs by a year.  委员会的拖沓使维修推迟了一年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hesitance and reluctance</td><td>/ˈhɛzɪtəns ənd rɪˈlʌktəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>犹豫与不情愿</td><td>met with hesitance and reluctance<br>遭到犹豫与不情愿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The offer was received with hesitance and reluctance.  这项提议遭到了犹豫和不情愿的对待。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>alibi</b></td><td>/ˈæləbaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不在场证明；托辞，借口</td><td>a watertight alibi<br>无懈可击的不在场证明</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two witnesses confirmed his alibi for that night.  两名证人证实了他当晚的不在场证明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defense</td><td>/dɪˈfens/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>辩护，辩解；防御</td><td>in his own defense<br>为他自己辩护</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He said nothing in his own defense at the hearing.  在听证会上他没有为自己作任何辩解。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 justification</td><td>/ˌdʒʌstəfəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>正当理由，辩解</td><td>no justification for it<br>毫无正当理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is no justification for treating them so harshly.  如此严厉地对待他们毫无正当理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 plea</td><td>/ˈpliː/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>辩解，抗辩；恳求</td><td>enter a plea of guilty<br>作出有罪答辩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She ignored his plea for more time and closed the file.  她无视了他要求延期的恳求，合上了卷宗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 reason</td><td>/ˈriːzən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>理由，原因；推理</td><td>a good reason for<br>…的充分理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave no reason for leaving the meeting early.  他没有给出提前离开会议的理由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>alienate</b></td><td>/ˈeɪliəneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>疏远，离间；使疏离</td><td>alienate one's supporters<br>疏远自己的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His harsh remarks alienated many longtime friends within a single evening.  他刻薄的言辞在一个晚上就疏远了许多老朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disaffect</del></td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈfekt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使离心</td><td>disaffected voters<br>心生不满的选民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The broken promises disaffected much of the party base.  这些空头承诺使党的许多基础支持者离心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 disgruntle</del></td><td>/dɪsˈɡrʌntl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使不满，使发怒</td><td>disgruntle the staff<br>使员工不满</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The sudden pay freeze disgruntled the entire department.  突然的薪资冻结让整个部门都不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sour</td><td>/ˈsaʊər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>使恶化，使变坏；酸的</td><td>sour the relationship<br>使关系恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The argument soured their friendship for years to come.  那次争吵使他们的友谊多年蒙上阴影。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unite</td><td>/juˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>联合，团结</td><td>unite against a threat<br>联合抵御威胁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crisis united the villagers as nothing else had.  这场危机以前所未有的方式团结了村民。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reunite</td><td>/ˌriːjuːˈnaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>重新团聚，重新联合</td><td>reunite with family<br>与家人团聚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The band reunited after twenty years apart and toured again.  乐队在分开二十年后重新组合，再度巡演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 reconcile</td><td>/ˈrekənsaɪl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使和解，协调</td><td>reconcile conflicting views<br>调和相互冲突的观点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is hard to reconcile these two versions of the story.  很难把这个故事的两种说法协调一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>align</b></td><td>/əˈlaɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>调准，校准；使结盟</td><td>align the wheels<br>校正车轮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mechanic aligned the front wheels in ten minutes.  修车师傅十分钟就把前轮调正了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 improperly adjusted</td><td>/ɪmˈprɑːpərli əˈdʒʌstɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>未调准的，调节不当的</td><td>improperly adjusted brakes<br>调节不当的刹车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The machine vibrated because the belt was improperly adjusted.  机器震动是因为皮带没调好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 irregular</td><td>/ɪˈreɡjələr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不规则的，不平整的</td><td>an irregular shape<br>不规则的形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path was made of irregular stones of many sizes.  这条小路由各种大小不规则的石头铺成。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>allay</b></td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 alleviate</td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 increase the intensity of</td><td>/ɪnˈkriːs ðə ɪnˈtɛnsəti əv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，使…强度增大</td><td>increase the intensity of training<br>加大训练强度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Adding fuel will increase the intensity of the fire within minutes.  加燃料会在几分钟内加大火势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exacerbate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæsərbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧，使恶化</td><td>exacerbate the crisis<br>使危机加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the budget would only exacerbate the housing shortage.  削减预算只会使住房短缺进一步恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>allegiance</b></td><td>/əˈliːdʒəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，效忠</td><td>swear allegiance to<br>宣誓效忠于</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New citizens pledge allegiance to the country at the ceremony.  新公民在仪式上宣誓效忠国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commitment</td><td>/kəˈmɪtmənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>致力，投入；承诺</td><td>a firm commitment to<br>对…的坚定承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed a deep commitment to protecting local wildlife.  她表现出保护当地野生动物的坚定投入。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dedication</td><td>/ˌdedəˈkeɪʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>献身，全心投入；奉献</td><td>dedication to one's work<br>对工作的全心投入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her dedication to her students earned her the teaching award.  她对学生的全心投入为她赢得了教学奖。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 piety</td><td>/ˈpaɪəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>虔诚；孝顺</td><td>filial piety<br>孝道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old woman was admired for her quiet piety and charity.  这位老妇人因其内敛的虔诚与善行而受人敬重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 faithfulness</td><td>/ˈfeɪθfəlnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>忠诚，忠实</td><td>faithfulness to a promise<br>信守承诺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was rewarded for years of faithfulness to the family business.  他因多年忠于家族企业而获得回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steadfastness</td><td>/ˈstedˌfæstnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>坚定不移，忠贞</td><td>steadfastness in adversity<br>逆境中的坚定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her steadfastness during the strike inspired the other workers.  她在罢工期间的坚定不移鼓舞了其他工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inconstancy</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːnstənsi/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>反复无常，不忠</td><td>the inconstancy of love<br>爱情的反复无常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His inconstancy made it impossible to rely on any promise he made.  他的反复无常使人无法信赖他的任何承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infidelity</td><td>/ˌɪnfɪˈdelɪti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>不忠，背信</td><td>marital infidelity<br>婚内不忠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The couple separated after years of quarrels and infidelity.  这对夫妻在多年争吵和背叛后分居了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 perfidiousness</del></td><td>/pərˈfɪdiəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>背信弃义，奸诈</td><td>the perfidiousness of an ally<br>盟友的背信弃义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians still debate the perfidiousness of the general's secret deal.  史学家仍在争论那位将军密约的背信程度。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 treachery</td><td>/ˈtretʃəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>背叛，变节</td><td>an act of treachery<br>一次背叛行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was executed for treachery after selling secrets to the enemy.  他因向敌人出卖机密犯下叛国罪而被处死。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>alleviate</b></td><td>/əˈliːvieɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缓和，减轻</td><td>alleviate the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A warm bath helped alleviate the soreness in his back.  热水澡帮他缓解了背部的酸痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ease</td><td>/ˈiːz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减轻，缓和；容易</td><td>ease the pain<br>减轻疼痛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The medicine eased her headache within minutes of the first dose.  这药在服下第一剂几分钟内就缓解了她的头痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mitigate</td><td>/ˈmɪtɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，使缓和</td><td>mitigate the damage<br>减轻损害</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Planting trees can mitigate the effects of summer heat.  种树可以减轻夏季高温的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 palliate</del></td><td>/ˈpælieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓和；掩饰</td><td>palliate the symptoms<br>缓解症状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new drug can only palliate the pain, not cure the disease.  这种新药只能减轻疼痛，无法治愈疾病。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 soothe</td><td>/suːð/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安慰，抚慰；减轻</td><td>soothe a crying baby<br>安抚哭闹的婴儿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soft music helped soothe the restless patient through the long night.  轻柔的音乐帮助安抚了焦躁的病人度过漫长的夜晚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 allay</td><td>/əˈleɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，平息</td><td>allay public fears<br>平息公众恐慌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The statement was meant to allay concerns about safety.  这份声明意在减轻人们对安全的担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 relieve</td><td>/rɪˈliːv/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减轻，缓解；换班替换</td><td>relieve the pressure<br>缓解压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short walk relieved the tension in her shoulders.  短暂散步缓解了她肩膀的紧张。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 increase the intensity of</td><td>/ɪnˈkriːs ðə ɪnˈtɛnsəti əv/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，使…强度增大</td><td>increase the intensity of training<br>加大训练强度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Adding fuel will increase the intensity of the fire within minutes.  加燃料会在几分钟内加大火势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exacerbate</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæsərbeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧，使恶化</td><td>exacerbate the crisis<br>使危机加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting the budget would only exacerbate the housing shortage.  削减预算只会使住房短缺进一步恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>allude</b></td><td>/əˈluːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>间接提到，暗指</td><td>allude to a problem<br>间接提到某个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She alluded to the scandal without ever mentioning any names.  她间接提到了那桩丑闻，却从未点名。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 imply</td><td>/ɪmˈplaɪ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暗示，含有…意思</td><td>imply a criticism<br>暗含批评之意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Silence at such a moment may imply consent rather than doubt.  此刻的沉默可能意味着同意而非怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 indicate</td><td>/ˈɪndəˌkeɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>表明，示意</td><td>indicate a preference<br>表明偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The survey results indicate a growing distrust of large banks.  调查结果表明人们对大银行的不信任在加深。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 infer</td><td>/ɪnˈfɜːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>推断，推论</td><td>infer from the evidence<br>从证据中推断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From his tone we inferred that the negotiations had failed.  从他的语气我们推断谈判已经失败。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insinuate</td><td>/ɪnˈsɪnjueɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>含沙射影地暗示；使巧妙混入</td><td>insinuate that someone lied<br>暗指某人说了谎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He insinuated that the accounts had been quietly altered.  他暗示账目被人悄悄改动过。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intimate</td><td>/ˈɪntəmeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>暗示，透露；亲密的</td><td>intimate one's intentions<br>透露自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The minister intimated that new rules would appear next month.  部长暗示下月将出台新规。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suggest</td><td>/səˈdʒest/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>暗示；建议</td><td>suggest an alternative<br>提出替代方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her hesitation suggested that she knew more than she admitted.  她的犹豫暗示她知道的比承认的更多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>allure</b></td><td>/əˈlʊr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>吸引，诱惑</td><td>the allure of fame<br>名声的诱惑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap rents allure young artists to this run-down district.  低廉的租金吸引年轻艺术家来到这个破旧街区。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 entice</td><td>/ɪnˈtaɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使，引诱</td><td>entice customers with discounts<br>用折扣吸引顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Free samples were used to entice shoppers into the new store.  免费样品被用来引诱购物者进入新店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seduce</td><td>/sɪˈduːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱使（背离正道），引诱</td><td>seduce someone into crime<br>引诱某人犯罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High salaries seduced many teachers away from the classroom.  高薪诱使许多教师离开了课堂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 solicit</td><td>/səˈlɪsɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>请求，征求；招揽</td><td>solicit donations<br>募集捐款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The charity solicited funds from local businesses all winter.  该慈善机构整个冬天向本地企业募捐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tempt</td><td>/ˈtempt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>诱惑，引诱</td><td>tempt fate<br>冒险行事，玩火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The smell of fresh bread tempted us into the little bakery.  新鲜面包的香味把我们诱进了那家小面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 captivate</td><td>/ˈkæptɪveɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使着迷，深深吸引</td><td>captivate the audience<br>深深吸引观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her first novel captivated readers around the world.  她的第一部小说令世界各地的读者着迷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enchant</td><td>/ɪnˈtʃænt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使入迷，使陶醉</td><td>be enchanted by the scenery<br>被风景迷住</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old fishing village enchanted every visitor who stayed there.  那个古老渔村令每位住过的游客陶醉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lead on</td><td>/liːd ˈɑːn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>引诱，误导（使抱幻想）</td><td>lead someone on<br>误导某人抱幻想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She finally realized he was leading her on with empty promises.  她终于意识到他在用空头承诺吊着她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unattractive</td><td>/ˌʌnəˈtræktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有吸引力的，难看的</td><td>an unattractive offer<br>没有吸引力的报价</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The job was well paid but otherwise thoroughly unattractive.  这份工作薪水不错，但其他方面毫无吸引力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ally</b></td><td>/ˈælaɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>同盟者，支持者；结盟</td><td>a close ally<br>亲密的盟友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Britain remained a close ally of the United States.  英国一直是美国的亲密盟友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supporter</td><td>/səˈpɔːrtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，拥护者</td><td>a loyal supporter<br>忠诚的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of supporters gathered outside the stadium before kickoff.  开赛前数千名支持者聚集在体育场外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 confederate</td><td>/kənˈfedərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>同盟者，同伙</td><td>a confederate in crime<br>犯罪同伙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The thief and his confederate were arrested the following morning.  那个小偷和他的同伙第二天早上被捕。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sympathizer</td><td>/ˈsɪmpəθaɪzər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，同情者</td><td>a known sympathizer<br>已知的同情者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Police questioned several sympathizers of the banned movement.  警方审问了几名被禁运动的支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adversary</td><td>/ˈædvərseri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌手，对手</td><td>a formidable adversary<br>强劲的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He respected his adversary even while fighting to win.  即使在拼命求胜时，他也尊重自己的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rival</td><td>/ˈraɪvəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>对手，竞争者</td><td>a bitter rival<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two firms have been rivals in this market for decades.  这两家公司在该市场竞争了数十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aloft</b></td><td>/əˈlɔːft/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>副词、形容词</td><td>在空中，在高处</td><td>hold a banner aloft<br>高举横幅</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The balloon stayed aloft for nearly three hours.  那只气球在空中停留了近三个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overhead</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈhed/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>副词、形容词</td><td>在头顶上（的），在上方（的）</td><td>an overhead light<br>顶灯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A helicopter circled overhead while we waited on the roof.  我们在屋顶等候时，一架直升机在上方盘旋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grounded</td><td>/ˈɡraʊndɪd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>在地面上的，停飞的</td><td>grounded by the storm<br>因暴风雨而停飞</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All flights were grounded because of the heavy fog.  由于大雾，所有航班都被迫停飞。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>aloof</b></td><td>/əˈluːf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，冷漠孤傲的</td><td>remain aloof from<br>对…保持冷淡疏远</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He stayed aloof from office gossip and rarely joined lunch.  他对办公室的闲话保持冷淡，很少参加午餐。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 detached</td><td>/dɪˈtætʃt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷漠超然的，不介入的</td><td>a detached attitude<br>超然冷漠的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She described the accident in a strangely detached voice.  她用一种异常冷漠的语气描述了那场事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 offish</td><td>/ˈɔːfɪʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，不友好的</td><td>seem a bit offish<br>显得有点冷淡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new neighbors seemed offish until we invited them over.  在我们邀请之前，新邻居显得有些冷淡。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unsociable</td><td>/ʌnˈsoʊʃəbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不爱交际的</td><td>an unsociable person<br>不爱交际的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long years of study had left him rather unsociable.  多年苦读使他变得相当不爱交际。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 withdrawn</td><td>/wɪθˈdrɔːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>内向孤僻的，不与人来往的</td><td>become withdrawn<br>变得孤僻内向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy grew quiet and withdrawn after moving to the city.  搬到城里后，那男孩变得沉默孤僻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 standoffish</td><td>/ˌstændˈɔːfɪʃ/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冷淡的，拒人千里的</td><td>a standoffish manner<br>拒人千里的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her standoffish greeting made the guests feel unwelcome.  她冷淡的招呼让客人觉得不受欢迎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sociable</td><td>/ˈsoʊʃəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好交际的，友善的</td><td>a sociable personality<br>好交际的性格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is far more sociable than her quiet older brother.  她比她安静的哥哥爱交际得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>altruism</b></td><td>/ˈæltruɪzəm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>利他主义，无私</td><td>an act of altruism<br>一次利他之举</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her donation was pure altruism, since she refused any publicity.  她的捐赠纯属无私，因为她拒绝任何宣传。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 egoism</td><td>/ˈiːɡoʊɪzəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>利己主义，自私</td><td>naked egoism<br>赤裸裸的利己主义</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His politics were driven by egoism rather than any principle.  他的政治行为出于利己主义而非任何原则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 egotistic</td><td>/ˌiːɡəˈtɪstɪk/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我中心的，自负的</td><td>an egotistic remark<br>自负的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director was too egotistic to accept advice from anyone.  这位主管太自负，不肯接受任何人的建议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-centered</td><td>/ˌselfˈsentərd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自我中心的，自私的</td><td>a self-centered child<br>自我中心的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is so self-centered that he never asks how others feel.  他极其自我中心，从不问别人的感受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 self-concerned</td><td>/ˌsɛlf kənˈsɜːrnd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>只顾自己的，自我关注的</td><td>narrowly self-concerned<br>只顾自己的狭隘心态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their proposal was too self-concerned to win wider support.  他们的提案过于只顾自身，难以获得更广泛支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 selfish</td><td>/ˈselfɪʃ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>自私的</td><td>for selfish reasons<br>出于自私的理由</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was selfish of him to take the last seat on the bus.  他抢占公交车最后一个座位真是自私。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amalgamate</b></td><td>/əˈmælɡəmeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>合并，混合</td><td>amalgamate two companies<br>合并两家公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two small colleges amalgamated to cut administrative costs.  两所小学院合并以削减行政成本。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mix</td><td>/ˈmɪks/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>混合，掺和</td><td>mix the ingredients<br>把配料混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Mix the flour and water until the dough becomes smooth.  把面粉和水混合，直到面团变得光滑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fuse</td><td>/ˈfjuːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，熔合</td><td>fuse two traditions<br>融合两种传统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her music fuses jazz rhythms with traditional folk melodies.  她的音乐把爵士节奏与传统民谣旋律融为一体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intermix</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪks/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>掺杂，混合在一起</td><td>intermix with water<br>与水混合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two populations intermixed freely over several centuries.  这两个族群在几个世纪里自由混居。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compound</td><td>/kəmˈpaʊnd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>混合，合成；使恶化</td><td>compound a medicine<br>配制药剂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pharmacist compounded an ointment from three simple ingredients.  药剂师用三种简单原料配制出一种药膏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meld</td><td>/ˈmeld/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>融合，合并</td><td>meld into one<br>融为一体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The choir's voices melded into a single warm sound.  合唱团的声音融合成一种温暖的声响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mingle</td><td>/ˈmɪŋɡl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>混合；与人交往</td><td>mingle with the crowd<br>混入人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Smoke mingled with fog above the crowded harbor.  烟雾与雾气在拥挤的港口上方交织。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 integrate</td><td>/ˈɪntəˌɡreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>整合，使成一体</td><td>integrate into the system<br>融入体系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school works hard to integrate new arrivals into class life.  学校努力让新来者融入班级生活。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intermingle</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈmɪŋɡl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>混合，交融</td><td>intermingle freely<br>自由交融</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 At the fair, tourists and locals intermingled all afternoon.  在集市上，游客和当地人整个下午都混在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 separate</td><td>/ˈsepəreɪt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>分开，分离</td><td>separate the yolks<br>把蛋黄分离出来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please separate the recyclable waste from the ordinary rubbish.  请把可回收垃圾与普通垃圾分开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 isolate</td><td>/ˈaɪsəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>隔离，使孤立</td><td>isolate a patient<br>隔离病人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors isolated the sick passengers as soon as the ship docked.  船一靠岸，医生就隔离了生病的乘客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ambiguous</b></td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doubtful</td><td>/ˈdaʊtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以确定的，可疑的</td><td>of doubtful value<br>价值可疑的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is doubtful whether the bridge can carry such loads.  这座桥能否承受如此重量还很难说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unclear</td><td>/ʌnˈklɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不清楚的，不明确的</td><td>remain unclear<br>仍不明确</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is still unclear who made the final decision.  谁做的最终决定仍不清楚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncertain</td><td>/ʌnˈsɜːrtn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不确定的，不明朗的</td><td>an uncertain future<br>不明朗的未来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The date of the meeting remains uncertain until funding is approved.  在资金获批前会议日期仍不确定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 distinct</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰的，明显的</td><td>a distinct impression<br>清晰的印象</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a distinct difference between the two dialects.  这两种方言之间有明显差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pellucid</td><td>/pəˈluːsɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>清晰明确的，易懂的</td><td>pellucid prose<br>明白易懂的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His pellucid explanation made the theory seem obvious.  他清晰的讲解让这个理论显得浅显易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 patent</td><td>/ˈpeɪtnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>显而易见的，明显的；专利</td><td>a patent lie<br>显而易见的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The excuse was a patent lie and everyone knew it.  这个借口是显而易见的谎言，人人都知道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 blatant</td><td>/ˈbleɪtnt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明显的，公然的</td><td>a blatant lie<br>赤裸裸的谎言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report exposed a blatant disregard for safety rules.  报告揭露了对安全规则的公然无视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 explicit</td><td>/ɪkˈsplɪsɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，明确的</td><td>explicit instructions<br>明确的指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave explicit instructions about how to store the samples.  她就样品的保存方式给出了明确指示。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lucid</td><td>/ˈluːsɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>表达清晰的，易懂的</td><td>a lucid explanation<br>清晰的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor offered a lucid summary of a difficult argument.  教授对一个艰深论证做了清晰的总结。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 perspicuous</del></td><td>/pərˈspɪkjuəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>表述清晰的，明白易懂的</td><td>a perspicuous style<br>明白易懂的文风</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His writing is perspicuous even when the topic is technical.  即使话题很专业，他的文章也清晰易懂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amble</b></td><td>/ˈæmbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>漫步，闲逛</td><td>amble along the beach<br>沿海滩漫步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We ambled along the river until the sun went down.  我们沿着河边漫步，直到日落。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ramble</td><td>/ˈræmbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>漫谈，东拉西扯；漫步</td><td>ramble on about<br>喋喋不休地扯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He rambled on about his childhood for a full hour.  他絮絮叨叨讲了整整一小时童年往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 saunter</td><td>/ˈsɔːntər/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>闲逛，漫步</td><td>saunter down the street<br>沿街闲逛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sauntered into the office half an hour late.  他迟到半小时，悠然地走进办公室。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stroll</td><td>/ˈstroʊl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>散步，闲逛</td><td>take a stroll<br>去散散步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They strolled through the old market after dinner.  晚饭后他们在老市场里闲逛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wander</td><td>/ˈwɑːndər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>漫游，闲逛；走神</td><td>wander around aimlessly<br>四处漫无目的闲逛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child wandered off while his parents were shopping.  孩子在父母购物时走开乱逛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dally</td><td>/ˈdæli/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>虚度时光，磨蹭</td><td>dally over lunch<br>吃午饭磨蹭半天</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Don't dally on the way home; dinner is almost ready.  回家路上别磨蹭，晚饭快好了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dawdle</td><td>/ˈdɔːdl/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>拖拖拉拉，磨蹭</td><td>dawdle on the way<br>路上拖拖拉拉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She dawdled over her homework until it was nearly midnight.  她做作业拖拖拉拉，一直到近午夜。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 step quickly</td><td>/stɛp ˈkwɪkli/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>快步走，加快脚步</td><td>step quickly across the road<br>快步穿过马路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We stepped quickly through the rain toward the waiting taxi.  我们在雨中快步走向等候的出租车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ambrosial</b></td><td>/æmˈbroʊziəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>特别美味的；芬芳的</td><td>an ambrosial dessert<br>美味无比的甜点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soup had an ambrosial aroma that filled the whole house.  这汤散发的美妙香气弥漫了整座房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 savory</td><td>/ˈseɪvəri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>美味的，可口的</td><td>a savory dish<br>一道可口的菜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She served a savory pie made with mushrooms and herbs.  她端上一道用蘑菇和香草做的美味派。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aromatic</td><td>/ˌerəˈmætɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>芳香的</td><td>aromatic herbs<br>芳香的草药</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The kitchen was full of aromatic spices from the market.  厨房里满是从市场买来的芳香香料。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perfumed</td><td>/pərˈfjuːmd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有香味的，洒了香水的</td><td>perfumed soap<br>香皂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She wrote the letter on thin perfumed paper.  她用薄薄的香纸写了那封信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 redolent</td><td>/ˈredələnt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>芳香的；令人想起…的</td><td>redolent of spices<br>散发香料气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old room was redolent of pipe smoke and leather.  那间旧屋弥漫着烟斗烟味和皮革气息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 odorous</td><td>/ˈoʊdərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有气味的，散发气味的</td><td>odorous flowers<br>散发气味的花</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The odorous blossoms attracted bees from the whole valley.  这些散发香气的花朵吸引了整个山谷的蜜蜂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fetid</td><td>/ˈfetɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的</td><td>fetid air<br>恶臭的空气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fetid smell rose from the blocked drain behind the house.  屋后堵塞的下水道散出恶臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 noisome</td><td>/ˈnɔɪsəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的；令人极厌恶的</td><td>a noisome odor<br>一股恶臭气味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Workers refused to enter the noisome tunnel without masks.  工人们不戴口罩不肯进入那条恶臭的隧道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 stenchy</del></td><td>/ˈstɛntʃi/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>臭气熏天的</td><td>a stenchy alley<br>臭气熏天的小巷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The stenchy garbage heap made the whole street unbearable.  臭气熏天的垃圾堆让整条街难以忍受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 malodorous</td><td>/ˌmælˈoʊdərəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>恶臭的</td><td>malodorous waste<br>恶臭的废弃物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Inspectors traced the malodorous discharge to a nearby factory.  检查人员追查到那股恶臭排放来自附近一家工厂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 rancid</td><td>/ˈrænsɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（油脂）腐臭的，变质的</td><td>rancid butter<br>变质的黄油</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The butter had turned rancid after a week in the heat.  黄油在高温下放了一周已经变质发臭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ameliorate</b></td><td>/əˈmiːliəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改善，改进</td><td>ameliorate the situation<br>改善局面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new subsidy did little to ameliorate rural poverty.  新补贴对改善农村贫困几乎没有作用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 improve</td><td>/ɪmˈpruːv/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>改善，提高</td><td>improve the quality of<br>提高…的质量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular practice quickly improved her pronunciation and her confidence.  经常练习很快改善了她的发音和自信。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 recuperate</td><td>/rɪˈkuːpəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>康复，恢复（健康或体力）</td><td>recuperate from an illness<br>病后康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He spent two weeks at the coast recuperating from surgery.  他在海边待了两周，从手术中康复。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aggravate</td><td>/ˈæɡrəveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加重，恶化；激怒</td><td>aggravate the situation<br>使局势恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Running on the injury will only aggravate it.  带着伤跑步只会让伤势加重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 worsen</td><td>/ˈwɜːrsən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使变坏，恶化</td><td>worsen over time<br>随时间恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her cough worsened after a week outdoors in the cold.  在寒冷的户外待了一周后，她的咳嗽加重了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deteriorate</td><td>/dɪˈtɪriəreɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>恶化，变坏</td><td>deteriorate rapidly<br>迅速恶化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her health deteriorated sharply during the cold winter.  在寒冷的冬天里她的健康急剧恶化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 damper</td><td>/ˈdæmpər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>抑制因素，扫兴的事物</td><td>put a damper on<br>给…泼冷水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain put a damper on our plans for the picnic.  这场雨给我们的野餐计划泼了冷水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amenable</b></td><td>/əˈmiːnəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的，服从的</td><td>amenable to suggestion<br>愿意接受建议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new manager is amenable to any reasonable suggestion.  这位新经理对任何合理的建议都乐于接受。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 compliant</td><td>/kəmˈplaɪənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>顺从的</td><td>a compliant employee<br>顺从的员工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children were unusually compliant during the long ceremony.  在漫长的仪式上，孩子们出奇地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 docile</td><td>/ˈdɑːsəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>温顺的，听话的</td><td>a docile animal<br>温顺的动物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old horse was docile enough for beginners to ride.  这匹老马很温顺，初学者也能骑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 submissive</td><td>/səbˈmɪsɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>a submissive attitude<br>顺从的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She spoke in a submissive tone that surprised her colleagues.  她以顺从的语气说话，令同事惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tractable</td><td>/ˈtræktəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易驾驭的，温顺的</td><td>a tractable student<br>听话的学生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The puppy proved surprisingly tractable during the first weeks of training.  这只小狗在最初几周的训练中出乎意料地听话。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obedient</td><td>/əˈbiːdiənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>服从的，顺从的</td><td>an obedient child<br>听话的孩子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dog sat quietly, obedient to every hand signal.  那只狗安静地坐着，服从每个手势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 intransigent</td><td>/ɪnˈtrænzɪdʒənt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不妥协的，固执的</td><td>an intransigent attitude<br>不妥协的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides remained intransigent, so the negotiations collapsed.  双方都毫不妥协，谈判因此破裂。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 contumacious</del></td><td>/ˌkɑːntuˈmeɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不服从的，倔强的</td><td>a contumacious witness<br>不服从的证人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contumacious witness was fined for defying the judge.  那名抗命的证人因违抗法官而被罚款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ungovernable</td><td>/ʌnˈɡʌvərnəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以控制的，无法管束的</td><td>an ungovernable temper<br>无法控制的脾气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crowd became ungovernable once the gates were opened.  大门一开，人群就变得无法控制。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unruly</td><td>/ʌnˈruːli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难驾驭的，不守规矩的</td><td>an unruly crowd<br>不守规矩的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two teachers struggled to calm the unruly class.  两位老师费力地安抚那个不守规矩的班级。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amenity</b></td><td>/əˈmenəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>便利设施；融洽，和谐</td><td>basic amenities<br>基本便利设施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hotel offers every amenity a business traveler could want.  这家酒店提供商务旅客所需的各种便利设施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 comfort</td><td>/ˈkʌmfərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>舒适；安慰</td><td>live in comfort<br>过舒适的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old chair gave him a great deal of comfort.  那把旧椅子给他带来极大的舒适。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convenience</td><td>/kənˈviːniəns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>便利，方便；便利设施</td><td>for the sake of convenience<br>为了方便起见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For convenience, we booked a hotel next to the station.  为方便起见，我们订了车站旁的酒店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affability</td><td>/ˌæfəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>和蔼可亲，友善</td><td>unfailing affability<br>始终如一的和蔼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His affability made even nervous newcomers feel at ease.  他的和蔼可亲让紧张的新人也感到自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 agreeability</del></td><td>/əˌɡriəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>宜人，令人愉快；随和</td><td>the agreeability of the climate<br>气候的宜人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The agreeability of the host made the long dinner pleasant.  主人的随和让这场长餐变得愉快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harmony</td><td>/ˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>和谐，融洽</td><td>live in harmony with<br>与…和谐共处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two communities have lived in harmony for generations.  这两个社区世代和睦相处。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accord</td><td>/əˈkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>一致，符合；授予</td><td>in accord with<br>与…一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His actions were in accord with the policy he announced.  他的行动与他宣布的政策一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 concord</td><td>/ˈkɑːŋkɔːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和睦</td><td>live in concord<br>和睦相处</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty brought decades of concord between the two states.  该条约给两国带来数十年的和睦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consonance</td><td>/ˈkɑːnsənəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>一致，和谐</td><td>in consonance with<br>与…相符一致</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her behavior was in consonance with her stated beliefs.  她的行为与她宣称的信念一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discordance</td><td>/dɪsˈkɔːrdəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不一致，不和</td><td>discordance between reports<br>各报告之间的矛盾</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is a clear discordance between the two witnesses' accounts.  两位证人的叙述之间存在明显不一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inharmony</del></td><td>/ɪnˈhɑːrməni/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不和谐，不协调</td><td>inharmony among members<br>成员之间不和</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Growing inharmony among members finally split the committee.  成员间日增的不和最终分裂了委员会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amiable</b></td><td>/ˈeɪmiəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好脾气的，友好易相处的</td><td>an amiable manner<br>和气友善的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our amiable guide answered the same question a dozen times.  我们和气的导游把同一个问题回答了十几遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friendly</td><td>/ˈfrendli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友好的，亲切的</td><td>a friendly welcome<br>友好的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shopkeeper was friendly and remembered every regular customer.  店主很友好，记得每位常客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affable</td><td>/ˈæfəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼的，温和的</td><td>an affable host<br>和蔼的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our affable neighbor greets everyone on the street by name.  我们和蔼的邻居在街上会叫出每个人的名字打招呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amicable</td><td>/ˈæmɪkəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友善的，和睦的</td><td>an amicable settlement<br>友好的和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute ended in an amicable agreement between neighbors.  这场纠纷以邻里间的友好协议告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disagreeable</td><td>/ˌdɪsəˈɡriːəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人不快的；脾气坏的</td><td>a disagreeable task<br>令人不快的任务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cleaning the drains was a thoroughly disagreeable job.  清理下水道是件极其讨人厌的活儿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-natured</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈneɪtʃərd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，凶恶的</td><td>an ill-natured remark<br>恶毒的言论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ill-natured comments about her work upset everyone at the meeting.  他对她工作的恶意评论让会上每个人都很不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-tempered</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈtɛmpərd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>脾气坏的，易怒的</td><td>an ill-tempered remark<br>一句脾气暴躁的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His ill-tempered remark silenced everyone at the dinner table.  他脾气暴躁的话让餐桌上所有人都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unamiable</del></td><td>/ʌnˈeɪmiəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友善的，不和气的</td><td>an unamiable manner<br>不友善的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unamiable silence made the other guests feel quite uncomfortable.  他不友善的沉默让其他客人感到很不自在。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 ungenial</del></td><td>/ʌnˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友善的；不宜人的</td><td>an ungenial climate<br>不宜人的气候</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ungenial landlord refused even to greet his tenants.  这位不和气的房东连招呼都不愿跟房客打。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ungracious</td><td>/ʌnˈɡreɪʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无礼的，不客气的</td><td>an ungracious refusal<br>不客气的拒绝</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed ungracious to refuse such a generous offer.  拒绝如此大方的提议似乎显得无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unpleasant</td><td>/ʌnˈplezənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人不快的，讨厌的</td><td>an unpleasant surprise<br>令人不快的意外</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was an unpleasant smell coming from the basement.  地下室传来一股难闻的气味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amicable</b></td><td>/ˈæmɪkəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友善的，和睦的</td><td>an amicable settlement<br>友好的和解</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dispute ended in an amicable agreement between neighbors.  这场纠纷以邻里间的友好协议告终。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friendly</td><td>/ˈfrendli/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>友好的，亲切的</td><td>a friendly welcome<br>友好的欢迎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shopkeeper was friendly and remembered every regular customer.  店主很友好，记得每位常客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affable</td><td>/ˈæfəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼的，温和的</td><td>an affable host<br>和蔼的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our affable neighbor greets everyone on the street by name.  我们和蔼的邻居在街上会叫出每个人的名字打招呼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amiable</td><td>/ˈeɪmiəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>好脾气的，友好易相处的</td><td>an amiable manner<br>和气友善的举止</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our amiable guide answered the same question a dozen times.  我们和气的导游把同一个问题回答了十几遍。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 genial</td><td>/ˈdʒiːniəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>和蔼亲切的；温和宜人的</td><td>a genial host<br>亲切的主人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our genial neighbor invited the whole street over for dinner.  我们和蔼可亲的邻居请整条街的人来吃晚饭。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antagonistic</td><td>/ænˌtæɡəˈnɪstɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敌对的，对抗的</td><td>antagonistic to reform<br>反对改革的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The union was openly antagonistic to the new management.  工会对新管理层公开敌对。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hostile</td><td>/ˈhɑːstəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敌对的，怀有敌意的</td><td>a hostile reaction<br>充满敌意的反应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The audience turned hostile as soon as he raised taxes.  他一提加税，观众立刻变得充满敌意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfriendly</td><td>/ʌnˈfrendli/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不友好的，冷淡的</td><td>an unfriendly stare<br>不友好的注视</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The waiter gave us an unfriendly look and walked away.  服务员不友好地看了我们一眼便走开了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>amity</b></td><td>/ˈæməti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>友好关系，亲善和睦</td><td>a treaty of amity<br>友好条约</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two nations signed a treaty of amity and commerce.  两国签署了友好通商条约。</td></tr>
</tbody>

<tr><td>↳近 friendship</td><td>/ˈfrendʃɪp/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>友谊，友好</td><td>a lasting friendship<br>持久的友谊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their friendship survived twenty years and three continents.  他们的友谊经历了二十年和三大洲的考验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enmity</td><td>/ˈenməti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌意，仇恨</td><td>long-standing enmity<br>积怨已久的仇恨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old enmity between the families still poisons village life.  两家的旧仇至今仍毒害着村里的生活。</td></tr>

<tr><td colspan="7">例 The plan met with open hostility from local residents.  该计划遭到当地居民的公开敌视。</td></tr>
